    Full character table: identity name description characterVersion culture; persona personality structured scenario appearance voice imagePrompt; prompts system postHistory prefill and insert as depthInjections zero only; greetings first and alternate text only; sampleChat examples; avatar portrait sprite visualKind; creator tags responseSchema; raw escrow. Persona decode encode rules preserve attribute maps and collapse multi-element text attributes to first element. applyBodyToCard always rebuilds persona from canonical.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook-1`)
    MemoryEntry uses entry not content, keywords array not CSV, and explicit weight placement plus priority eviction. Maps title content comment enabled constant triggers secondaryTriggers selectiveLogic position sortOrder priority probability excludeRecursion and book scanDepth tokenBudget globalRecursion. lorebookType is other; per-entry caseSensitive matchWholeWords scanDepth are null. weight to sortOrder and priority to priority match Agnai memoryEntryToNative interop comments verified locally for the local mapping.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Escrow is original keyed by format id: original.agnai for character and original agnai-lorebook for books. Export deep-clones parsed raw or baseCard then applies canonical values and pretty-prints JSON; unmapped keys like extensions _id userId survive in the clone. Character set is two-state unconditional write or delete string array without twin decode compare; malformed non-string skipped values stay. Persona always rebuilds so multi-element text attributes collapse to first element even same-format. Lorebook entryToWire is a three-state diff by id Map so untouched entries preserve parsed fields, including selectiveLogic; JSON formatting may normalize. Optional book fields preserve twin presence. Embedded characterBook is Agnai MemoryBook not CCv3: extractLorebook uses memoryBookToCanonical identically to standalone; fromCanonical re-embeds via applyLorebook twin-overlay; no book means no key; empty bundle deletes stale characterBook; readPieces uses this override not shared CCv3 extractor.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Two-state character overlay versus three-state lorebook overlay: same well-formed result different mechanism. Persona always rebuilt; multi-element text persona collapses on every round-trip. Detection firewall is absence of schemaVersion and body not their presence. imageSettings affixes reconcile per key with sampler provider knobs surviving from twin clone. insert is single fixed-depth slot; only depthInjections zero re-encodes; role origin enabled stay undefined on decode. Embedded characterBook is Agnai-native MemoryBook via adapter extract apply not shared character-book mapper. selectiveLogic is ST-import residue on ST numeric convention never authored by Agnai editor so fresh cross-format encode never emits it.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Authoritative files: character adapter index.ts for detect toCanonical fromCanonical extractLorebook; memory book lorebook.ts and shared mappers; coverage.ts; shared character-book soft-import path and lore-enums selectiveLogic; convert and adapter extractLorebook EmitContext for bundles; character and lorebook entity schemas; canonical escrow; samples/agnai and samples/lorebooks/agnai; Agnai common types paths cited as AGPL interop facts only with no code copied.

### Backyard format

- id: `reference/formats/backyard`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/backyard.md`
- Developer reference for Backyard.ai Faraday adapters: two unrelated character shapes in one folder src/formats/backyard exporting backyard legacy flat JSON with aiName era writing .json and byaf ZIP archives with manifest character scenarios and images writing .byaf. Neither is a Tavern superset. Detection Legacy JSON scores 0.9 on Backyard-unique string keys aiName aiPersona aiDisplayName customDialogue, 0.55 weak persona-only fallback guarded against description and Agnai kind character, else 0. BYAF scores 1 only when bytes are ZIP PK with manifest characters and scenarios arrays and characters zero string, no weak tier. Field map Legacy uses firstStr alias lists and brace placeholder conversion on text fields; models two names display versus char shorthand nickname; leaves most CharacterBody unset beyond mapped slots. Field map BYAF maps archive via archiveToBody with verbatim text no brace conversion, multi-scenario alternate greetings with deterministic byaf path ids, media data URIs, and loreItems sampler transcript non-primary fields escrow only. Escrow and round-trip cover original.backyard with non-bijective toTavern toBackyard fieldOut unedited raw preserve, original.byaf full archive twin packByaf applyBody media and greeting path resolution refuse hostile ids, and from-scratch minimal shell. Quirks cover two-names fix, no version fabrication, lossy mature rating, one character per archive, titled versus untitled alt homes, BYAF detect no weak tier, creator tags bypass conversion. Source of truth lists adapter container scenario coverage sample files.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Registry keeps highest detect above 0.5. Legacy JSON detect reads only input.text: 0 if no text non-JSON or non-object; 0.9 if any of aiName aiPersona aiDisplayName customDialogue is a string; 0.55 if persona is string description is undefined and kind is not character so it misses flat cards pairing persona with description and Agnai cards with kind character; else 0. Both scores sit below 1.0 RoleCall Risu Agnai and the 0.9 Tavern reader on shared inputs; 0.55 clears threshold barely by design. BYAF detect reads only input.bytes: 0 unless ZIP PK signature then unzips only manifest.json requiring characters and scenarios arrays and characters zero a string; match scores 1 with no weaker tier because schema-shaped manifest ZIP is unambiguous.
  - **H3 Legacy JSON** (`legacy-json`)
    Text-only detect with two tiers: 0.9 on Backyard-unique string keys, 0.55 persona-only with description undefined and kind not character, else 0. Scores intentionally below 1.0 specialists and sit at or under generic Tavern 0.9 on shared inputs.
  - **H3 BYAF** (`byaf`)
    Bytes-only detect: ZIP PK then manifest.json with characters and scenarios arrays and first character id string scores 1; any failure returns 0. No ambiguous middle tier in the registry.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Legacy JSON maps identity name nickname description version personality scenario systemPrompt firstMessage exampleMessages creator tags via first non-empty alias lists; converted text fields run brace placeholder conversion; nickname carried only when aiName differs from resolved name. Unmapped CharacterBody slots stay unset and survive only in escrow. BYAF maps through byaf-container then archiveToBody: displayName name persona narrative formattingInstructions firstMessages exampleMessages images background attribution discovery rating; text is verbatim without brace conversion; alternate greetings fold extra primary firstMessages and later scenarios with deterministic byaf path ids; loreItems sampler settings chat transcript and non-primary scenario fields stay escrow only.
  - **H3 Legacy JSON** (`legacy-json-1`)
    Canonical table for legacy wire aliases: name from aiDisplayName aiName displayName name; nickname from aiName when distinct; description personality scenario systemPrompt greetings examples creator tags. Converted fields run toTavern on import. Everything else in CharacterBody left unset; other source keys survive only through escrow. Two Backyard name keys are distinct authored fields modeled as name versus nickname.
  - **H3 BYAF** (`byaf-1`)
    archiveToBody maps displayName name persona narrative formattingInstructions firstMessages examples images background author timestamps isNSFW rating. persona.personality left unset because BYAF has no personality description split. Alternate greetings from extra primary firstMessages and secondary scenarios with byaf path ids for stable re-import. Media re-embedded as data URIs; missing image files skipped. loreItems not mapped to lorebook refs; sampler runtime transcript and non-primary scenario extras stay escrow only.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  original.backyard holds whole parsed raw for legacy; original.byaf holds full opened archive base64. Legacy placeholders single-brace to Tavern double on import via toTavern with lookaround safe char; inverse toBackyard only handles char and user so is lossy. fieldOut re-emits raw when unedited so non-bijection cannot corrupt untouched cards; edited fields write lossy inverse. BYAF export rebuilds archive from twin, applyBodyToArchive, packByaf; sampler transcript grammar ride untouched. Media unedited versus edited split; clearing images deletes archive files. Alternate greetings match by stable scenario path id with hostile id refusal; orphan secondary scenarios deleted; titled title match only without resolvable id. From-scratch character synthesizes minimal manifest character scenario shell without requiring a source archive.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Two names two keys: displayName name versus aiName nickname; earlier fold destroyed distinct aiName and was fixed with regression WB-1; BYAF applyBody same rule. No version fabrication at parse; export invents 1.0 only for from-scratch without twin. BYAF boolean isNSFW is lossy toward mature never produced on import; export only writes for exact explicit or all-ages else leaves twin; from-scratch defaults false unless explicit. One character per archive characters zero only per ahoylabs cap. Untitled alts fold into primary firstMessages; titled alts become secondary scenario files; retitle alone does not move homes. BYAF detect has no weak tier. creator and tags bypass placeholder conversion on legacy.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Authoritative files: legacy adapter index.ts for detect toCanonical fromCanonical plus coverage.ts; BYAF adapter byaf.ts; ZIP container open pack original twin in byaf-container.ts; scenario and greeting id mapping in byaf-scenarios.ts; byaf-coverage.ts; canonical.ts for escrow and id policy; samples/backyard ahoylabs byaf conformance archives; legacy JSON shape fixtured only in backyard.test.ts because no clean public sample was found.

### JanitorAI format

- id: `reference/formats/janitor`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/janitor.md`
- The external importer mapping is a co-developer inspection dated 2026-10-24; current tests prove emitted files against that mapping, not a live external import. JanitorAI format is the developer reference for Bunzai's export-only Janitor target. Janitor has no character file and no official card export; its only importer hides in the avatar upload on create_character and reads a PNG chara tEXt chunk. The folder src/formats/janitor default-exports the janitor character codec, writing .png only, and janitor-lorebook, the SillyTavern world info codec under Janitor's id for the Scripts editor's Import JSON. Both return 0 from detect, so Janitor-bound PNGs import through the SillyTavern reader, and toCanonical delegates to that reader. The importer ignores ccv3, JSON and charx, recognizes CCv3 not at all, and on its V1 branch maps top-level description to Personality and top-level personality to the public Description, so a plain ST card lands swapped. The adapter builds the normal V2 card through the Tavern codec, rewrites the six top-level mirror fields the way Janitor reads them, keeps data an ordinary V2 card, embeds chara only, uses the inline portrait or else the imported source PNG, and reports dropped fields plus warnings for an empty definition, a missing first message or a PNG over 10 MB.

- **H2 What Janitor's importer reads** (`what-janitors-importer-reads`)
  Janitor's importer reads only the chara keyword: ccv3 is ignored, JSON and charx are refused, and the avatar input takes images up to 10 MB. Checks run in order Text-gen-webui, TavernAI Character with all six top-level keys present, Character.AI shapes, then Character Card V2; CCv3 is not recognized. On the V1 branch top-level description fills Personality, the definition, and top-level personality fills the public Description bio; on the pure V2 branch data.description lands in the bio. Every recognized field is written even when empty, and alternate greetings, tags, creator notes, character_book, system prompt and post-history instructions are dropped.
- **H2 What the adapter writes** (`what-the-adapter-writes`)
  fromCanonical builds the normal V2 card through v2CardObject in card-png.ts, so a V3 card yields its V2 backfill, then sets six top-level mirrors: name, description as data.description plus data.personality joined by a blank line for Janitor's Personality, personality as creator_notes for the public bio, scenario, first_mes and mes_example. data stays an ordinary V2 card readable by SillyTavern. charaOnlyPng embeds under chara alone, carried by the inline PNG portrait or else the PNG the card was imported from; a card with neither is refused naming janitor. The report names dropped fields from coverage.ts and warns on an empty definition, a missing first message, or a PNG over the 10 MB avatar limit.
- **H2 Lorebooks** (`lorebooks`)
  Janitor lorebooks are Scripts of type lorebook, separate objects linked to bots. The Scripts editor imports SillyTavern world info JSON, its tooltip key importSillyTavernFile, so janitor-lorebook is the ST worldbook codec under Janitor's id. Linked lorebooks never travel inside the PNG and the export dialog says so.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  The source-of-truth table names where each claim on this page lives. src/formats/janitor/index.ts is the character adapter: it builds the V2 card, applies the Janitor-specific handling, embeds the chara chunk, and attaches the serialize report with its warnings; it also default-exports the lorebook codec beside it. src/formats/janitor/coverage.ts declares which canonical paths the Janitor importer keeps, which drives the dropped list in every report and the export dialog's honesty lines. src/formats/_shared/card-png.ts holds v2CardObject and charaOnlyPng, shared with the other chara-only host, including the fallback from the portrait to the imported source PNG. src/formats/_shared/host-worldbook.ts wraps the SillyTavern worldbook codec under a host id. src/formats/janitor/janitor.test.ts pins the chunk keywords, field placement, refusals and report contents.

### Lumiverse format

- id: `reference/formats/lumiverse`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/lumiverse.md`
- Developer reference for four Lumiverse adapters: an ST CCv2/v3 character wire with Lumiverse extensions and an optional modules ZIP, a versioned regex export, an account Persona object, and a block-based preset wrapper that exports as a flat ST preset. There is no dedicated lorebook codec; embedded character_book uses the shared bundle layer. Detection, field mapping, bounded archive handling, dual escrow twins, module packing, character variants, regex and persona behavior, and the preset wrapper's import-only asymmetry are documented with their source files.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Registry highest score above 0.5; ties resolve by registration order not content. Character checks ZIP first: modules json scores 1; bare module.risum scores 0 ceding to Risu; card.json with Lumiverse fingerprint scores 0.96; PNG chara ccv3 fingerprint 0.95; plain V3 V2 flat JSON fingerprint 0.95. Fingerprints include expressions expression_groups alternate_fields alternate_avatars lora name world_book_ids databank_ids ttsVoice. Fingerprinted cards outbid ST 0.9; plain ST without fingerprint scores 0 here. A genuine modules ZIP ties Risu at 1 because Risu claims any ZIP with card.json, but sorted folder registration puts Lumiverse before Risu and the strictly-greater registry comparison retains Lumiverse. Regex claims only type lumiverse_regex_scripts with scripts array score 1. Persona claims name description and both subjective and objective pronoun strings score 0.95 outbidding ST character heuristic 0.9.
  - **H3 Character** (`character`)
    Character detection checks modules ZIP first, cedes bare module.risum, then requires Lumiverse fingerprints for charx, PNG, or JSON. Modules ZIP ties Risu, but sorted registration and first-winner tie handling select Lumiverse.
  - **H3 Regex** (`regex`)
    lumiverse-regex claims only self-identifying envelope type lumiverse_regex_scripts with scripts array for score 1. Bare scripts array or wrong type scores 0. No other adapter reads this shape.
  - **H3 Persona** (`persona`)
    Claims object with name description and both subjective_pronoun and objective_pronoun as strings at 0.95. Pronoun pair is the signal that outbids SillyTavern flat character name plus description at 0.9.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Character field map reuses shared tavern-fields dataToBody applyBodyToData and CCv3 assets with lumiverse dialect identical to ST emotion typing. Lumiverse-specific data lives under extensions as native not escrow but almost none lifts to body: alternate_fields and alternate_avatars bridge to body.variants via altsToVariants label merge and variantsToAlts wholesale overwrite; resolved expression and avatar data URIs become media.assets emotion or other. Other extension keys stay Workbench native panel only. Archive regex scripts decode to RegexRule arrays under extensions but never lift to behaviorRefs. Modules sidecar rehydrate merges expressions groups alternate fields avatars world_books regex_scripts into extensions with resolveAssetRef 64 MiB cap leaving bare paths for missing or oversized files.
  - **H3 Character** (`character-1`)
    Shared ST data mapping plus two bridges: alternate fields and avatars to CharacterVariant overrides by label; resolved expression group and avatar URIs to media assets. Remaining extension keys stay native. Module regex lands as typed rules under extensions without behaviorRefs. Variants export overwrites alternate_fields and alternate_avatars wholesale from body.variants deleting keys when empty.
  - **H3 Modules sidecar** (`modules-sidecar`)
    rehydrateCardData merges lumiverse_modules.json into cloned extensions: expressions and expression_groups produce emotion assets when resolved; alternate_fields feed variants; alternate_avatars produce other assets; world_books and regex_scripts land on private extension keys with regex decoded to RegexRule arrays. resolveAssetRef turns archive paths into data URIs under 64 MiB or leaves bare path strings.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Two escrow buckets: original.sillytavern with hydrated extensions raw, unmapped variant, and optional PNG sourceMedia; original.lumiverse with modules, fromZip, and a base64 files map containing every ZIP entry, including card.json and lumiverse_modules.json. SillyTavern raw extensions are hydrated and not byte-identical to input card.json when imported from ZIP. Export clones the ST twin, overlays body and media, merges current extensions, folds variants, applies lorebooks, and chooses the requested or source container. packModulesFromExtensions rewrites the two structured JSON entries, uses deterministic asset paths and typed regex encoding, and preserves other files from the complete twin map. JSON export from a ZIP can conservatively refuse when original archive content would be lost. Plain-JSON unedited round trips are field-lossless; ZIP round trips are data-lossless rather than path- or byte-identical. Cross-format output carries canonical content while both twins remain escrowed.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  All ZIP inflate uses unzipBounded CARD_ARCHIVE_BOUNDS shared with Risu and BYAF: 96 MiB archive, 512 entries, 64 MiB per entry, 96 MiB aggregate. Regex codec converges account RegexScript export and reduced archive regex_scripts on one RegexRule with extras sealing account fields; placement maps to phases including memory; unmapped placements and substitution modes are not fabricated. Persona maps title, pronouns, knowledge refs, and image URL; its twin seals folder, default, narrator, and metadata. A from-scratch persona materializes required description, subjective, and objective string keys while optional possessive stays absent. alternate_character_name is a prompt override. Lumiverse preset import converts blocks to ST preset wire; export writes ST flat preset and does not reproduce the wrapper. The .lvbak importer accepts account, character, and preset regex scopes; scoped rows need a usable scope_id, while unknown or incomplete scopes fail per row without widening, leaving supported sibling rows importable.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Authoritative files: character adapter index.ts for detect toCanonical fromCanonical and container selection; modules.ts for rehydrate pack and fingerprint; variants-bridge for alternate fields and avatars to body.variants; regex.ts for both wire shapes; persona.ts; preset.ts; coverage.ts; shared tavern-fields assets png card-io; core archive registry and canonical; samples/lumiverse rich.extensions.card.json.

### Marinara format

- id: `reference/formats/marinara`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/marinara.md`
- Developer reference for four Marinara adapters: bare API data for regex scripts and personas plus native export envelopes for lorebooks and prompt presets. Tavern-shaped characters use SillyTavern while Marinara-only fields remain in the editor lens. Regex detection rejects overlapping ST rows before bidding 0.95; persona detection requires identity, authored text, and a theming/stat signal; unique lorebook and preset envelopes have structural gates. Field maps cover RegexRule, PersonaBody, native lorebook book/entry/folder semantics, and preset sections/groups/choices/parameters. Regex export rebuilds from canonical rules and extras. Persona, lorebook, and preset export overlay mapped changes onto sealed twins, retaining engine-only data. Empty persona sections are omitted on import, explicit clears survive export, and avatarPath is neither fabricated nor retained after an explicit clear. Lorebook nested folders and native entry exotica remain escrowed; preset string-encoded booleans, JSON fields, and unmapped variables/parameters survive.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Detection follows the registry rule that every adapter's detect runs and the highest score above 0.5 wins. Regex bids 0.95 after four ordered gates over a non-empty object array: reject ST scriptName, require id and findRegex on every row, and require a Marinara signal among targetCharacterIds, order, or string placement. Persona bids 0.95 only with string identity and text plus nameColor, boxColor, or personaStats. Lorebook bids 1.0 for the unique marinara_lorebook envelope with data.entries. Preset bids 0.95 for marinara_preset with data.preset and data.sections; groups and choiceBlocks are optional, and raw database string encodings are accepted.
  - **H3 Regex** (`regex`)
    detect returns 0.95 for a recognizable Marinara dump and 0 otherwise. The score is 0.95 not lower because SillyTavern's regex codec claims 0.9 on the shared findRegex replaceString shape, and even the ST-form fixture is named marinara-essentials. readMarinaraRows fails closed on the first of four gates: non-empty plain-object array; any scriptName rejects the whole file as ST dialect; every row needs string id and findRegex; at least one row needs targetCharacterIds, order, or placement ai_output or user_input. Real ST rows always carry scriptName so this adapter scores 0 and ST keeps its files.
  - **H3 Persona** (`persona`)
    detect returns 0.95 for a recognizable Marinara persona object and 0 otherwise. readMarinaraPersona requires all three: string id and name; string description and personality; and at least one theming or stat signal among nameColor, boxColor, or personaStats. The codec comment calls this combination disjoint from Lumi ST and RoleCall so a plain id name description object fails before any name-based disambiguation is needed.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook`)
    marinara-lorebook scores 1.0 only for the unique marinara_lorebook envelope with object data and an entries array. Version, metadata, and folders remain tolerant payload fields rather than detection requirements.
  - **H3 Preset** (`preset`)
    marinara-preset scores 0.95 only for marinara_preset with object data.preset and array data.sections. Groups and choices are optional; string booleans and JSON-string nested fields match the engine export.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Field map covers all four codecs. Regex maps each row to one RegexRule and derives set name from filename. Persona maps authored text, optional sections, brief, and image while sealing ids, themes, stats, crop, tags, and timestamps. Lorebook maps book budgets and recursion, plain-string keys with one useRegex flag, selective/matching/injection/timing/group/filter/source fields, order to sortOrder, and folder rows to flat categories while preserving nested parents. Preset maps sections to prompts, groups, choice blocks, supported samplers, and API options; string booleans and JSON strings are decoded, while conversation/game prompts, variables, metadata, and unmapped parameters remain in escrow.
  - **H3 Regex** (`regex-1`)
    The wire row is MarinaraRegexScript, one row per canonical RegexRule. id maps to id, name to label, findRegex to find as bare pattern without delimiters, flags and replaceString to flags and replace, trimStrings empty as undefined, placement ai_output user_input to phases output input with dedup order preserved, promptOnly true to targets prompt, depths passthrough, targetCharacterIds to characterIds empty as undefined, enabled and order to enabled and sortOrder, createdAt updatedAt to extras only. The regex set itself has no Marinara wire home: name derives from filename; description and set-level enabled never touch a Marinara file.
  - **H3 Persona** (`persona-1`)
    The wire object has no spec or envelope name; shape recognition is the Detection gate. name maps to name, description to content as the full first-person user voice, comment to brief when non-empty after trim, appearance personality backstory to sections appearance personality history when non-empty, avatarPath to presentation.imageUrl when truthy, and attribution.source is always synthesized as the literal marinara because the wire has no attribution field. id scenario theming stats crop tags timestamps have no canonical slot and ride sealed in escrow.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook-1`)
    The native lorebook envelope carries one book row, entry rows, and folder rows. Book mapping covers name, description, category, tags, enabled state, scan depth, recursion, token budget, and entry limit. Entries map plain primary and secondary keys plus one useRegex flag, selective logic, match settings, position/depth/role, order as canonical sortOrder rather than priority, timing, groups, folder, probability, recursion, character filters, and additional scan sources. Folder rows project to categories; nested parentFolderId and other engine-only fields survive in escrow.
  - **H3 Preset** (`preset-1`)
    Preset section rows map to prompts with role, enable and marker state, group, ordered or depth placement, XML wrapping, override refusal, and marker slot. Group rows carry parent, order, and enabled. Choice blocks carry question, variable, cardinality, JSON-string options, separator, random pick, and sort order. The parameters JSON string maps supported samplers and squash-system, show-thoughts, and reasoning-effort API options. Conversation/game prompts, variables, author metadata, and unsupported parameters remain on the raw twin.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Escrow is the canonical wrapper original field keyed by format id. Regex seals the imported row array but rebuilds output from canonical rules and extras. Persona clones its twin and overwrites mapped fields; hub ids and Marinara business ids remain separate. Lorebook and preset also clone complete native envelopes, match rows by stable ids, and patch only mapped differences, retaining nested folder state, engine-only entry fields, preset variables, unsupported parameters, and metadata. Fresh entities emit minimal native envelopes. These are structural JSON round trips; whitespace and object-key order are not promised.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Quirks pin behavioral edges. Marinara regex detection outbids the overlapping SillyTavern shape through four gates. Whitespace-only persona sections are omitted on import, while an explicit empty section survives export. A fresh persona does not fabricate avatarPath; clearing an imported canonical image deletes the twin's key, while unchanged absence stays absent. Character cards still use the SillyTavern adapter and expose Marinara extras only through the editor lens. No dedicated Marinara design spec exists in this checkout.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth table maps regex to index.ts, persona to persona.ts, lorebooks to lorebook.ts, and presets to preset.ts. Shared regex mapping, filename naming, JSON readers, the character-side editor lens, canonical escrow, entity schemas, format tests, and the SillyTavern-dialect regression fixture are also listed.

### NovelAI format

- id: `reference/formats/novelai`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/novelai.md`
- NovelAI format is the developer authority for how Bunzai's single novelai-lorebook codec detects, maps, and round-trips native NovelAI lorebook exports through the canonical model. NovelAI has no character card, so the family is one lorebook reader writer under formats novelai writing .lorebook JSON. Detection scores 1.0 only when lorebookVersion is numeric and entries is an array, scoring zero on ST object-keyed entries, Risu wrappers, or version alone. Field map covers NAI entry names versus LorebookEntry including keys as inline slash pattern flags, forceActivation as constant, searchRange as scanDepth, budgetPriority as sortOrder, plus nested Enums are strings on the wire, the searchRange unit caveat, the contextConfig assembly block, and Phrase bias loreBiasGroups. Escrow and round-trip seals the whole raw book under original novelai-lorebook and overlays by uuid or index with merge replace and category rules, including Cross-format into NovelAI no twin shells. Quirks list no book name, dual bias casing, non-clearable categories, priority never serializes, no character adapter, and nothing executed. Source of truth maps codec schema fixtures samples and tests. No design spec exists; content is read from adapter tests and samples.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  detect returns 1.0 when parsed JSON has a numeric lorebookVersion and an array entries, otherwise 0. Unlike SillyTavern's generic 0.9 which yields the ceiling to a more specific reader, nothing else claims this exact pair so 1.0 is the plain top score not a collision defense. Three shapes score zero: ST worldbooks with object-keyed entries, Risu exports wrapped with type risu and no top-level lorebookVersion, and lorebookVersion alone without entries. Detection does not sniff the version number; 3 4 and 6 all pass the same typeof number check because only entry-level richness differs across versions not the top-level shape.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Field map documents NovelAI entry names versus LorebookEntry. keys use inline slash pattern flags via shared keywordToTrigger with forceRegex false so bare keys stay literal. text becomes content, displayName title, forceActivation constant, searchRange scanDepth, budgetPriority alone is sortOrder and is placement not eviction, categories become body.categories, book name and budgets stay empty or zero defaults. Secondary ST axes like selective recursion probability and position are fixed defaults because NAI lacks them. Nested subsections cover Enums are strings on the wire for trim and insertion fields, The searchRange unit caveat on character versus message units, The contextConfig assembly block for prefix suffix budgets and insertionPosition, and Phrase bias loreBiasGroups as first-class canonical with dual casing accepted.
  - **H3 Enums are strings on the wire** (`enums-are-strings-on-the-wire`)
    trimDirection, insertionType, and maximumTrimType are plain strings in both real samples on hand, a v3 slice and a v6 export. The schema records them as an open string union rather than a closed enum on the assumption NAI may add values. The codec does no int-to-string decoding for these three fields; whatever string the export carries is read and written as-is.
  - **H3 The `searchRange` unit caveat** (`the-searchrange-unit-caveat`)
    NAI searchRange is measured in characters of story text; SillyTavern scanDepth is measured in messages. Both map to canonical scanDepth as the closest home for how far back to scan, and the exact number round-trips losslessly through escrow on a same-format NAI trip. Cross-format import into a messages-based format carries the number verbatim with no unit conversion, so NAI 1000 characters becomes 1000 messages downstream. That is documented drift not a bug and does not affect same-format NAI round-trip.
  - **H3 The `contextConfig` assembly block** (`the-contextconfig-assembly-block`)
    EntryContextConfig is read field-by-field skipping absent wire keys so deep equality against the twin re-decode stays stable. Mapped fields are prefix suffix tokenBudget reservedTokens trimDirection insertionType maximumTrimType and insertionPosition as a signed offset within the target context section, a different axis than canonical position and depth. budgetPriority is excluded from this block and lives in sortOrder alone so it has exactly one canonical home.
  - **H3 Phrase bias (`loreBiasGroups`)** (`phrase-bias-lorebiasgroups`)
    LoreBiasGroup arrays are first-class canonical not escrow. The reader tolerates two casings for whenInactive generateOnce and ensureSequenceFinish snake and camel. The real v6 sample confirms dual casing for generateOnce and ensureSequenceFinish while whenInactive appears only camel; the v3 fixture has no loreBiasGroups. Mapped fields include enabled bias phrases sequence sequences type and the three booleans. bias is signed strength defaulting to zero if missing or non-numeric; phrase type is NAI's numeric enum carried open.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Escrow and round-trip store only the whole raw lorebook at original novelai-lorebook raw with no unmapped or sourceMedia. fromCanonical overlays the body onto that twin matching entries by id on v6 uuid or by array index on v3 v4. Flat scalars replace when changed; nested blocks differ: contextConfig merges so twin-only dials survive and you cannot unset by omit; loreBiasGroups is replace-or-delete and edits drop snake_case duplicates while untouched entries keep dual casing; categories can be added renamed or toggled but not bulk-cleared by empty list. Twin-only fields include lorebookVersion top-level order lastUpdatedAt category subcontext settings and advancedConditions. Tests pin v3 field-level fidelity and v6 bias and category edits with 4-space JSON. Cross-format into NovelAI no twin builds each entry on a blank NAI shell with default contextConfig and lorebookVersion 3.
  - **H3 Cross-format into NovelAI (no twin)** (`cross-format-into-novelai-no-twin`)
    A canonical lorebook from SillyTavern Risu or CCv3 has no NAI twin, so each entry is built onto a valid shell instead of overlaid. contextConfig defaults match a real NAI blank-entry export: empty prefix, newline suffix, tokenBudget 2048, reservedTokens 0, budgetPriority from sortOrder, doNotTrim, newline insertion, sentence maximumTrimType, insertionPosition -1. searchRange defaults to 1000 and the book writes lorebookVersion 3. v3 v4 entries carry no wire id so cross-format entries get none; a v6 uuid only survives on a same-format twin.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Quirks pin edges already detailed under Escrow. The book has no name field so body.name is always empty and canonicalId falls back to character for the entity id. contextConfig writes merge while loreBiasGroups writes replace and dual bias casing only survives untouched entries. Categories cannot be bulk-cleared through this codec. Priority never serializes: NAI is single-axis budgetPriority placement and a regression test greps that the string priority never appears in a serialized entry. No character adapter and no bundle path exists because NAI has no character card. Nothing is ever executed; the codec is pure JSON transform and advancedConditions always empty so far rides the twin only.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth maps concerns to files. The native lorebook codec for detect field map escrow and twin overlay is formats novelai lorebook.ts; folder default export is index.ts. Canonical lorebook schema including EntryContextConfig LoreBiasGroup and LorebookEntry is entities lorebook schema.ts with field meanings in entities lorebook.md. Shared keyword slash pattern flags decode is formats shared character-book.ts. Committed v3 fixture is reformatted 4-space test-only JSON; real untouched v6 sample is samples novelai crystal dragon with provenance in SOURCES.md. Adapter tests are lorebook.test.ts; canonical wrapper escrow and id policy are core canonical.ts.

### Pygmalion format

- id: `reference/formats/pygmalion`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/pygmalion.md`
- Pygmalion format is the developer authority for how Bunzai's single pygmalion character codec detects, maps, and round-trips the classic flat five-field character JSON and its PNG chara chunk through the canonical model. The wire is legacy snake_case char_name char_persona char_greeting world_scenario example_dialogue as standalone .json or PNG tEXt, not Tavern-lineage CCv2. Detection scores 1.0 after rejecting CCv2 vaud-json Agnai and Backyard shapes, then requiring classic field counts or char_name plus char_persona, with registry 1 beating SillyTavern's PNG chunk-only 0.9. Field map places persona into personality with description mirror, scenario greeting and examples, portrait from PNG carrier, empty prompts attribution discovery, and export overlay that prefers personality over description. Escrow and round-trip seals whole raw plus optional sourceMedia under original.pygmalion with no unmapped block, clones for lossless five-key overlay, and treats inline PNG portrait as carrier authority. Quirks cover display-only description mirror, never-invented name alias, overlay never clears, persona-required minimal detect, metadata in raw only, narrow PNG output, and no sibling codecs. Source of truth lists adapter coverage shared PNG helpers samples and tests.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Detection follows registry highest score above 0.5. detectPygmalion returns 1 for a recognizable classic card and 0 otherwise, claiming full confidence because no sibling shares its wire vocabulary. isPygmalionCard rejects known shapes first: spec or data for CCv2 v3, schemaVersion or body for vaud-json, kind character for Agnai, aiName or aiPersona for Backyard. Positive signal needs at least two of char_persona char_greeting world_scenario or the minimal char_name plus char_persona; name plus greeting alone is not enough. readCardJson decodes PNG chara ccv3 or raw JSON so the same scan applies to both carriers. ST PNG scores 0.9 from chunk presence alone so registry 1 beats 0.9 on real Pygmalion PNGs; reverse path returns 0 when decoded payload is not Pygmalion shape so real ST PNGs are never misclaimed.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Field map wires the five classic keys into CharacterBody. char_name with name fallback becomes identity.name defaulting empty. char_persona maps to persona.personality and is mirrored into identity.description on import only for description-only UIs. world_scenario becomes persona.scenario, char_greeting greetings.firstMessage, example_dialogue examples.exampleMessages. PNG carrier pixels become media.portrait and may re-export as the PNG container when the portrait is inline PNG; metadata has no canonical slot and rides original raw. prompts attribution and discovery are always empty objects. Export applyBodyToCard writes only when canonical is not undefined; char_persona prefers personality and falls back to description only if personality is undefined so description-only edits after import do not reach the wire. Legacy name alias is written back only if the source already had a name key.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Escrow stores under original.pygmalion the whole parsed card as raw plus sourceMedia b64 for PNG input only. There is no unmapped entry because Pygmalion has one wire shape. fromCanonical clones raw when escrow exists or starts from baseCard five empty strings then overlays via applyBodyToCard so unedited same-format trips are byte-for-byte including metadata. Portrait authority is the current canonical media.portrait: inline PNG becomes chara-embedded .png output; clearing produces JSON; explicit JSON drops portrait; unsupported refs fall back to honest JSON; explicit PNG without usable inline PNG fails. Cross-format carries prose and usable inline PNG only; metadata stays escrowed and is not copied into other apps; remote non-PNG portraits are reported dropped not fetched.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Quirks pin behavioral edges. Description mirror is display-only: import sets both personality and description from char_persona but export prefers personality so description-only edits never reach the wire. name alias is never invented; classic char_name-only cards stay that way. Overlay writes never clear: undefined canonical leaves the last wire value unlike Tavern three-state overlay. Minimal detection needs persona specifically so name plus greeting alone fails. metadata tool provenance rides raw only. PNG output is deliberately narrow with no remote fetch or non-PNG transcode. One codec only: no Pygmalion lorebook persona or regex sibling because the classic wire never carried those concepts.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth maps concerns to files. Character adapter detect toCanonical fromCanonical live in formats pygmalion index.ts; coverage declaration is coverage.ts. Shared card JSON reader for PNG chara ccv3 or raw text is formats shared card-io.ts; shared PNG chunk read write is png.ts. Canonical wrapper escrow original and id policy are core canonical.ts. Samples live under samples pygmalion classic.native.json. Tests are pygmalion.test.ts. Research provenance is reference platform-native-fields.md under the Pygmalion heading.

### Formats

- id: `reference/formats/README`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/README.md`
- Index of every format bunzai reads and writes. A format is a folder in src/formats name discovered automatically by the loader; each format family gets one reference page under this directory with architecture for discovery context. Coverage matrix points exclusively to generated FORMAT-SUPPORT.md from the live registry via bun run matrix with matrix:check failing CI on drift; a hand-maintained matrix here rotted once by missing three shipped adapters and will not be repeated. Reference pages table ships dedicated docs for SillyTavern RoleCall RisuAI Backyard Agnai NovelAI JanitorAI Saucepan vaud-json and Marinara with their covered kinds; adapters without a dedicated page yet such as pygmalion lumiverse chub and image-pack (a ZIP of pictures read as a Sprite pack) live in source folders and the matrix. Detection scores are detect confidence with higher winning and more-specific formats outranking generic Tavern; recognition threshold is 0.5. Cross-cutting links character-book extraction and re-embed. Not yet built planned tracks honesty gaps: Wyvern lorebook needs no codec because there is no verifiable standalone wire, only CCv2 PNG character export with Lexicon either in extensions.wyvern escrow or in-app only; wire-gate forbids codecs from wiki-inferred zero-byte formats. Lumiverse World Books need no dedicated codec either: import accepts only Risu-native and CCv3 entries shapes already emitted by risu-lorebook and sillytavern-lorebook; export is the same; the native lorebook entry is a database row not a portable file except inside .lvbak account backup as a separate multi-entity concern.

- **H2 Coverage matrix** (`coverage-matrix`)
  The exhaustive adapter list lives only in FORMAT-SUPPORT.md, generated from the live registry with bun run matrix; CI fails if it drifts under matrix:check. A hand copy in this README rotted once by missing three shipped adapters and does not get a second chance.
- **H2 Reference pages** (`reference-pages`)
  Family table links dedicated pages: SillyTavern for character v2/v3 png/json world info personas; RoleCall for character lorebook regex persona; RisuAI for charx native lorebook risum modules regex; Backyard Faraday for legacy json and byaf archive; Agnai for character and memory book; NovelAI for lorebook; Vaudeville native vaud-json for the canonical wrapper; Marinara for native lorebook prompt preset persona regex and characters as CCv3 plus extension fields. Adapters without a dedicated page yet, pygmalion lumiverse chub image-pack, are documented by source under src/formats and appear in the generated matrix. Detection scores are confidence each detect returns for its own format: higher wins; more-specific formats outrank the generic Tavern reader on the same card; threshold to be recognized at all is 0.5. JanitorAI and Saucepan have dedicated export-only PNG and lorebook references.
- **H2 Cross-cutting** (`cross-cutting`)
  Points to concepts/character-book.md as the single cross-cutting reference for an embedded character_book: how a card lorebook is extracted on import and re-embedded on export. That shared bundle concern is not restated on every family page, so adapters that ship cards with inline books should follow the character-book concept doc rather than inventing a second path.
- **H2 Not yet built (planned)** (`not-yet-built-planned`)
  Tracks planned honesty so the matrix separates exists from coming. Wyvern lorebook: no standalone wire format and no dedicated codec; Wyvern character export is CCv2 PNG already readable; Lexicon is character-attached so it rides extensions.wyvern escrow via CCv2 or stays in-app like Janitor with no serialized file; zero real public export means wiki-inferred codecs would violate the wire-gate doctrine that a codec exists only when a real wire serializes it; revisit only if a real standalone export lands with open questions on standalone serialization and CCv2 extensions. Lumiverse World Books: already covered by risu-lorebook and CCv3/ST worldbook codecs; LumiRealm parseDirectLorebook accepts only Risu type risu data array and CCv3 entries object; export returns Risu-shaped loreBook arrays; the native lorebook entry is a database row not a portable file; only .lvbak full-account backup would carry it as multi-entity restore, not a lorebook codec. A native LumiWorldBookEntry file codec would be dead surface.

### RisuAI format

- id: `reference/formats/risu`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/risu.md`
- RisuAI format is the developer authority for how Bunzai's risu family detects, maps, and round-trips .charx character cards, native lorebook exports, and regex script sets through the canonical model. The folder exports three codecs: risu character for .charx ZIP with card.json assets and optional module.risum, risu-lorebook for native lore JSON, and risu-regex for .risum or bare customscript rows writing JSON only. Detection covers Character via PK magic and card.json at 1.0 with bounded unzip, and Lorebook via type risu data array at 1 or character_book extract at 0.75. Field map reuses shared Tavern data mapping plus first-class extensions.risuai behavior media and book extract for Character, and native key secondkey alwaysActive insertorder folder modes for Lorebook. Escrow and round-trip seals original.risu with raw assets module and safety flags and overlays lorebook twins; same-format is data-lossless not byte-identical ZIP. Quirks document always CCv3, millisecond dates, selective media cross-format, behavior never executed, risum edit-blocked re-encode, risu-regex scores and rows-only export, native role, and insertorder as sortOrder. Source of truth lists adapters rpack helpers and samples. Regex codec detail lives mainly under Quirks.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Detection splits Character and Lorebook. Character detect returns 1.0 only for a ZIP whose first two bytes are PK and whose bounded inflate finds a card.json entry; bare JSON never wins, and spec contents are not inspected so V2-shaped data inside still scores 1.0. Every inflate uses unzipBounded CARD_ARCHIVE_BOUNDS with 96 MiB archive and entry caps rejecting oversized input before decompress. Lorebook returns 1 for top-level type risu with array data, unambiguous against ST object entries, and 0.75 for portable character_book extracts so real Risu native always outranks the shared fallback.
  - **H3 Character** (`character`)
    detect returns 1.0 for a recognizable .charx and 0 otherwise via two steps: zip magic first two bytes PK or score zero immediately so bare JSON is never claimed; then a bounded single-entry inflate only card.json scores 1.0 if present else 0. Detection does not parse spec or spec_version; a charx with V2-shaped data still scores 1.0. Inflates use core unzipBounded with CARD_ARCHIVE_BOUNDS max archive 96 MiB, 512 entries, 64 MiB per entry, 96 MiB aggregate inflated, rejecting oversized entries before decompress.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook`)
    risu-lorebook returns 1 for the native envelope, 0.75 for a portable character_book extract, and 0 otherwise. Score 1 requires a top-level object with type exactly risu and an array data; that shape does not collide with SillyTavern worldbook object-keyed entries. Score 0.75 is a Chub-style or card-extracted character_book with entries array of CCv3-shaped rows, the same fallback ST lorebook accepts, scored below native so a real Risu export always wins any tie.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Field map covers Character then Lorebook. Character card.json data uses the shared Tavern V2 V3 mapper identical to SillyTavern; Risu delta is first-class extensions.risuai for license bias additionalText display settings imagePrompt vits customScripts triggerscripts virtualscript backgrounds variables prebuilt assets module toggles and privileged lowLevelAccess, plus millisecond date unit fix, assets to media roles, and character_book as bundle extract not Tavern map. Lorebook maps native loreBook rows where key and secondkey are comma strings, alwaysActive is constant, insertorder is sortOrder not priority, folder is categoryId, mode folder rows become categories, and book-level name settings stay empty because Risu keeps them on the character record.
  - **H3 Character** (`character-1`)
    card.json data maps through shared tavern-fields dataToBody and applyBodyToData like SillyTavern. Risu delta is extensions.risuai fully first-class: license bias additionalText settings viewScreen largePortrait inlay utilityBot lorePlus imagePrompt from sdData and newGenData, vits voice, customScripts triggerscript virtualscript backgrounds defaultVariables prebuiltAsset moduleToggles and privileged as lowLevelAccess warning never execution. Dates above 1e11 normalize ms to seconds on import and restore raw ms on same-format export. assets map to media roles icon main portrait emotion outfit pose background other with bytes in escrow. character_book is bundle extract via knowledgeRefs and re-embed on export; resolved empty list removes both slots rather than keeping a stale twin.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook-1`)
    Native field names differ from CCv3: key and secondkey are comma-joined strings split trimmed with slash pattern decode and useRegex force. comment maps to title and comment, alwaysActive to constant, activationPercent to probability, insertorder to sortOrder never priority, role kept as native-richer system user assistant, folder to categoryId, enabled always true. mode folder loreCache bookVersion ride escrow; mode folder rows lift to LorebookCategory. Envelope has no book name or settings so name empty and budgets zero. Native and charx-embedded Risu lore must canonicalize identically matching character-book insertorder path.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Character twin is original.risu with raw full card.json envelope, unmapped assetFiles base64 for every non-card non-risum zip entry, moduleRisum bytes, optional structured OpenedRisumModule, and hasExecutableContent plus privileged safety flags. Export clones raw or baseCard V3, applies Tavern three-state overlay, media merge, risuai twin-diff, restores raw millisecond dates, re-stringifies card with 4-space JSON, restores asset bytes, re-emits unedited module.risum or refuses edited, and re-zips. Data is lossless including assets and module but output bytes need not equal input due to JSON and zip regeneration. Lorebook twin original risu-lorebook raw walks rows by id for field-for-field unedited re-emit, partial rewrite on edit, drop on delete, append on add; book-level name depth budget are not written because Risu keeps them on the character record.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Quirks pin Risu-specific edges. Always CCv3 never V2 via wrapV3 and baseCard. Millisecond dates normalize per field above 1e11 because samples mix units. Media crosses format selectively for data http and ccdefault refs; archive-relative refs need twin uri match; same-format keeps assets via assetFiles. Behavior is first-class editable never executed and never re-emitted by non-Risu codecs. module.risum is RPack opened into structured view; unedited re-exports original bytes; edited throws RpackEditedExportBlockedError until fixtures flip the verified gate. risu-regex detects 0.8 for risum with regex and 0.85 for bare customscript arrays while charx stays character; export is pretty JSON rows never repacked module; phases map editinput output display and request spellings with open unlisted types. Native role is richer than embedded path; insertorder maps to sortOrder for clean ST order conversion.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth maps concerns to files. Character adapter detect container escrow dates and safety flags live in formats risu index.ts; risuai scalar mapping in risu-fields.ts; native lorebook in lorebook.ts; regex codec in regex.ts. RPack container codec table module and index live under formats risu rpack; structured module open is open-module.ts. Shared Tavern field map assets and character-book helpers are formats shared. Coverage is coverage.ts; canonical escrow core canonical.ts; bounded zip core archive.ts. Samples under samples risu with SOURCES.md.

### RoleCall to SillyTavern presets

- id: `reference/formats/rolecall-to-sillytavern`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/rolecall-to-sillytavern.md`
- How a RoleCall preset becomes a SillyTavern preset, separating what converts mechanically from the remainder needing human judgment. A RoleCall preset can carry a second program beside its prompts: a hook machine writing variables as text passes through, and conditionals reading them back. The page is one crossing on purpose while the machinery is not, since macros resolve through a canonical operation hub and no engine pair is written down anywhere. Converting without a decision: separator differences, indexed access lowering onto composed names, conditionals preserved rather than flattened, and macros nested in blocks or passed as arguments. Kit reports facts without deciding them, in a structure section carrying arrays, domains, wideVariables, pushHooks, choices and markerCandidates, plus stateLayer as read, absent or unknown. Four patterns follow: a choice group becomes a seeded variable, still author work; an inline cleanup macro is removed by guarding the appends that made it necessary, since regex scripts run over messages rather than prompt content and cannot replace it; a closed domain expands into a conditional, reported rather than applied; and a splice macro is promoted into a marker block by splitting its prompt. Hook machines are rendered as regex rules in declaration order, with authored values verbatim, while push is refused. Evidence is labelled unit-proven, integration-proven or code-read.

- **H2 What converts without a decision** (`what-converts-without-a-decision`)
  Handled in code, appearing in the transfer receipt only as rewrite entries. Separator differences resolve because each catalog declares the shape of its own forms, so roll with double-colon becomes roll with single colon without either engine knowing about the other. Indexed access lowers because SillyTavern has no arrays but an array of fixed slots is a set of variables whose names carry the index: getvarkey on propp_plan at 3 becomes getvar on propp_plan_3, and a computed index becomes a composed name, which works because a nested macro resolves before its consumer. SillyTavern documents exactly this shape itself. Conditionals are preserved rather than flattened because SillyTavern's current macro engine has if, else, closing tags, inversion and comparison; the target's own catalog decides this through the canonical flow.conditional operation, and an engine without conditionals still gets blocks flattened to their body with the condition kept as a comment. Macros nested inside a preserved block, or passed as arguments to a portable macro, are translated too; both were defects at one point and both are now covered by tests.
- **H2 What Kit reports** (`what-kit-reports`)
  The engine computes facts and does not decide what to do about them. A preset transfer or export receipt carries a structure section: arrays answering which variables are addressed by index, which literal indices appear, and how many accesses use a computed index; domains for variables whose every written value was a short literal, with the value set; wideVariables for variables that are literal but too long or too many to expand inline, named rather than dropped in silence; pushHooks for hooks that append to a list, with the trigger regex verbatim; choices, each choice macro resolved against the choice group it names with that group's declared options and default; markerCandidates for dead macros whose name resembles a canonical marker slot; and stateLayer as read, absent, or unknown. stateLayer deserves attention because a RoleCall hook machine lives only in escrow and never in the canonical body, so a piece whose escrow was dropped reports unknown rather than zero hooks, since zero read as there are none is precisely the mistake this distinction exists to prevent.
- **H2 Pattern 1: a choice group becomes a seeded variable** (`pattern-1-a-choice-group-becomes-a-seeded-variable`)
  The choice macro asks RoleCall for the option a user picked at load time, and SillyTavern has no such concept, so the macro is removed on the way out and the receipt records it. The choice group itself survives conversion as canonical data, so the receipt can hand you its options. Replace the read with an ordinary variable and seed that variable once at the top of the preset: a seed block placed first sets the variable to a value, and every former choice read becomes a plain getvar. The seed is the group's declared default when it has one. A person switching options edits one line instead of hunting every reference, which is the closest thing to the original behaviour a preset without a picker can offer.
- **H2 Pattern 2: fix the producer, not the consumer** (`pattern-2-fix-the-producer-not-the-consumer`)
  The regex macro rewrites a value as a prompt renders and SillyTavern has no such macro. Regex scripts are not the counterpart: they run over chat messages and world-info entries, never over preset prompt content, so no arrangement of them reproduces it and emitting one gives a rule that runs, matches nothing and looks correct. The move is to stop needing it, and the converter now performs that repair for the shape it recognises. A list assembled by appending separator plus item onto an empty variable comes out with a leading separator; guarding each append on whether the variable already holds something means it never appears, and the cleanup is deleted rather than translated. Scope travels with the variable, since chat-local and global live in separate stores. Nothing is compiled: recognition is textual, and a cleanup that rewrites rather than strips, reads something other than a plain variable, or whose producer is elsewhere comes back unfixed with a reason.
- **H2 Pattern 3: a closed domain expands into a conditional** (`pattern-3-a-closed-domain-expands-into-a-conditional`)
  The upper macro has no SillyTavern equivalent. Applied to a variable whose values are unknown it is unfixable; applied to one whose value set is closed it is a lookup. This is why the receipt reports domains: when every write is a short literal the full value set is known, so the uppercase form can be written beside the value, after which the upper read becomes a plain getvar. Check the receipt first, because the pattern needs a literal assignment site and only works for a variable listed under domains; in the preset measured here upper was applied to a computed variable, where the fix is Pattern 2. Domains counts every write form, not just setvar: shorthand assignment, addvar, incvar and decvar all open a domain, since a value derived from the current one cannot be enumerated. Three variables there looked like the closed set containing zero until the arithmetic writes driving them were counted; they are counters, and expanding one into a constant would badly mislead.
- **H2 Pattern 4: a macro becomes a marker prompt** (`pattern-4-a-macro-becomes-a-marker-prompt`)
  The message_history macro splices the transcript; SillyTavern does the same job with a marker prompt, a prompt-list entry named chatHistory that the builder fills during assembly. There is no macro to rewrite it to, so the conversion is a structural edit and the converter now performs it: the block carrying the macro is split, keeping the text before and after on either side of a new marker block. Order of operations is the whole trick, since the translator removes a macro with no home on the target and would leave nothing to promote; promotion therefore reads dead tokens off the original body and restructures first. Promotion happens only when the slot is unambiguous: exactly one shared-word match promotes, zero or several leave the token for a person, because content moved to the wrong slot reads as odd behaviour rather than a conversion error. A macro the target can still run is never touched. Every split is reported under promotions.
- **H2 The hook machine** (`the-hook-machine`)
  RoleCall's macro_engine_yaml is regex-triggered hooks: a trigger, flags, a placement of user_input or ai_output, an optional strip, and actions that set, unset, append or push. They line up with SillyTavern regex scripts, whose replacement is macro-substituted after capture groups fill in, so a replacement containing a setvar really does write state. A dialect-changing crossing now returns hookRules: the hooks as rules the target can run, in declaration order, triggers verbatim because both engines share regex syntax. The authored value crosses verbatim too, which is load-bearing: RoleCall writes templates woven around two capture groups and writes an empty value to clear, so guessing would discard a group or turn a clear into a write. sortOrder preserves declaration order because a hook that clears state must precede the hooks that write it. Push is still refused, since its rule count comes from how the preset reads the array. Bunzai still seals the YAML as escrow and never executes it, and placing the rules into a target remains a separate act.
- **H2 Evidence** (`evidence`)
  Claims are backed at different strengths and the difference matters. Unit-proven covers separator rewriting, indexed-access lowering, block preservation, nested-argument translation, every field of the structure report, the observations in explanation, the rendered hookRules, marker promotion and producer repair. Integration-proven covers a RoleCall preset crossing to SillyTavern through the real adapter registry, resolving its dialect from escrow and carrying structural findings, promotions and rendered rules into the receipt. Code-read covers SillyTavern's regex replacement path calling substituteParams, its placement values, its marker prompt identifiers, and the surfaces regex scripts actually run over, all read from that project's source. Pattern 1 remains a recommendation and says so. Patterns 2 and 4 were recommendations when the page was written and are now performed; Pattern 3 is reported rather than applied, since expanding a closed domain is still the author's edit.

### RoleCall format

- id: `reference/formats/rolecall`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/rolecall.md`
- Developer reference for seven RoleCall adapters: CCv2/v3 character cards with extensions.rolecall, a v1 lorebook envelope, the flat compendium export, persona and regex JSON exports, a RoleCall-flavored flat preset, and a preset file carrying a Script Sheet. Character detection bids 1.0 only for the RoleCall extension and steps aside for persona exports; lorebook detection recognizes its dedicated wrapper without colliding with SillyTavern world info, and the compendium codec reads and writes RoleCall's flat compendium file (raw entry rows, tree and constraints kept) without the v1 codec claiming it. Character mapping covers identity details, titled greetings, depth injections, presentation, media, and embedded lorebooks. Lorebook mapping flattens RoleCall's nested trigger, matching, injection, timing, grouping, scan-source, and advanced settings into canonical entries. Character export overlays canonical edits onto the sealed source while preserving native fields, including a documented depth-injection write asymmetry. Lorebook export preserves source dates and conditionally writes budget fields. Persona handling keeps brief and content distinct across two supported shapes. Regex handling models the live chat pipeline with open placement values. Preset handling shares the flat SillyTavern grammar but requires RoleCall fingerprint fields and preserves native YAML as sealed data. The source table identifies all seven codecs, shared helpers, schemas, design specs, and constructed samples.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Detection follows registry highest score above 0.5. Character detect returns 1.0 for CCv2 or V3 cards that carry extensions.rolecall whose type is not the literal persona, outranking SillyTavern 0.9 on the same card. Cross-kind firewall: RC persona exports reuse the CCv2 V3 shape with type persona so character returns 0 and toCanonical throws pointing at rolecall-persona instead of tying. Lorebook detect returns 1.0 for a RoleCall v1 export envelope with schemaVersion containing 1.0.0 as a substring and a lorebook object with an entries array; exportDate is optional. No firewall is needed versus ST because ST has a top-level entries object and no lorebook wrapper.
  - **H3 Character** (`character`)
    detect returns 1.0 for a CCv2 or V3 card carrying extensions.rolecall and 0 otherwise. Requirements are spec chara_card_v3 or chara_card_v2, a data object, and rolecallExt whose type is not the literal persona. Score 1.0 outranks SillyTavern generic 0.9. Cross-kind firewall: RC persona-export route emits the same CCv2 V3 shape with type persona; character detect returns 0 and toCanonical throws pointing at rolecall-persona so the adapters never tie at 1.0 on the same file.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook`)
    detect returns 1.0 for a RoleCall v1 export envelope with a schemaVersion string containing 1.0.0 and a lorebook object whose entries is an array, else 0. exportDate is optional for detection and import. The version match is a substring rather than exact equality, so beta tags pass; a nonmatching version scores zero even though the direct structural parser does not hard-reject it. The envelope does not collide with a SillyTavern worldbook, which has a top-level entries object without a lorebook wrapper, so no firewall is needed between the two lorebook codecs.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Field map covers Character RC deltas then Lorebook nesting. Character reuses shared Tavern data mapping and adds extensions.rolecall tagline genre fandom content_rating mapped all_hours late_night after_dark to all-ages mature explicit, source_url creators_note, details full_name title age pronouns, titled alternate greetings, prompt_depth_injections concatenated with depth_prompt origin, presentation casting-card colors fieldOrder background media_links, and assets with expression as emotion; unmapped loadout links ride escrow. Lorebook flattens RoleCallEntryV1 nested groups triggers matching injection priority timing grouping advanced scanSources characterFilter and unsupportedFields ST toggles into flat LorebookEntry; book settings metadata categories map at book level; scanPreset has no v1 wire slot.
  - **H3 Character** (`character-1`)
    Shared Tavern data mapping is documented on sillytavern; this table is RC layer only. extensions.rolecall maps tagline genre fandom content_rating with exact three-way rating, source_url creators_note, details full_name title free-text age pronouns, alternate_greeting_titles merged by index into alternate greetings, prompt_depth_injections as origin rolecall_details concatenated before depth_prompt, and presentation signature gradient palette fieldOrder background spoiler media_links. assets use shared assetsToMedia dialect rolecall with expression as emotion; PNG carrier bytes live in original.rolecall.sourceMedia. character_book is shared bundle re-embed; empty resolution removes stale twin book. id nsfw image urls loadout recommendations trackerPreset linked lists ride whole-card escrow.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook-1`)
    Canonical LorebookEntry is flat; RC v1 wire nests each entry into named groups and the codec flattens on import and re-nests on export. Groups cover triggers mode primary secondary selectiveLogic, matching case whole-words scanDepth, injection position depth role, priority sortOrder priority, timing sticky cooldown delay, grouping name category weight, advanced recursion memo ignoreBudget, scanSources four RC toggles, top-level characterFilter probability sideEffects metadata, and unsupportedFields for ST-origin vectorized groupOverride useGroupScoring automationId matchCharacterDepthPrompt matchCreatorNotes as first-class canonical. Book settings metadata categories map at book level; scanPreset has no RC v1 wire slot.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Character twin stores whole parsed card in original.rolecall.raw plus PNG sourceMedia when input was PNG. Export clones raw or fresh V3, applies Tavern overlay, media dialect rolecall, then applyBodyToRcExt three-state for presentation fields gradientColors palette background fieldOrder distinguishing untouched edited cleared while rebuilding nested details; scalars write when defined and cleared values on twin stay rather than delete. Depth-injection asymmetry: import concatenates rolecall_details and depth_prompt origins but export only rewrites depth_prompt via shared applyBodyToData; applyBodyToRcExt never writes prompt_depth_injections so details array re-emits from twin. Lorebook twin original rolecall-lorebook raw overlays by id; budgetMode entryBudget write only when twin had them or from-scratch differs from defaults; exportDate preserved from twin and only regenerated from-scratch, which differs from source product always minting fresh exportDate in the design spec.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Quirks pin RC edges and summarize preset, Script Sheet, persona and regex codecs. rolecall-script-sheet reads a preset file carrying script_sheet, or the older promptbook key, as a Script Sheet, outranking rolecall-preset, and writes the sheet back with script_sheet beside its blocks. rolecall-preset shares SillyTavern's flat grammar but requires native fingerprint keys; a from-scratch export writes a neutral empty choice_groups array so it stays distinguishable and self-readable. V3 source as object array is unread by shared Tavern string[] decoder so object form rides twin; greeting titles omit key when all untitled; background keeps customUrl or backgroundId slot; real sprites use type expression mapped to emotion. Persona codec claims 1.0 on rcpersona envelope and chara_card_v2 lookalike with type persona; brief never swaps with content; sections compile or fold unfold description; after_dark omitted when not mature explicit; no rcpersona PNG keyword path in code despite design specs. Regex codec models live find_pattern pipeline only at 0.9 for single script with rules; placements map to phases open-union; no substituteFind on this wire; unknown keys ride extras. coverage.ts is editor lens only not round-trip.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth maps character to index.ts and lorebook, persona, regex, and preset codecs to sibling files. Shared Tavern mapping, lore enums, assets, PNG and card readers, coverage, canonical escrow, persona schema, design specs, and constructed samples are also listed.

### Saucepan format

- id: `reference/formats/saucepan`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/saucepan.md`
- The external importer mapping is a co-developer inspection dated 2026-10-24; current tests prove emitted files against that mapping, not a live external import. Saucepan format is the developer reference for Bunzai's export-only Saucepan target. Saucepan's Companion Importer at labs/companion-importer supports SillyTavern PNG v1 and v2 only, reading the chara tEXt chunk; its Backup JSON is not confirmed against a real file, so there is no Saucepan reader. src/formats/saucepan default-exports the saucepan character codec, writing .png, and saucepan-lorebook, ST world info for the Lorebook Importer; both return 0 from detect and the character codec's toCanonical delegates to the SillyTavern reader. The importer's fixed default map merges description personality and scenario into Information, sends creator_notes to Description, greetings to Scenarios, keeps examples name and tags, and discards system prompt, post-history, character_book and extensions; the result is a private draft and oversized fields are clipped with a toast. The adapter writes the normal V2 card under chara alone with no field rearranged, carried by the portrait or else the imported PNG, and saucepanWarnings reports every field over Saucepan's code-point limits plus a hint to map discarded prompts to Formatting Instructions by hand. Lorebooks are chapters with no keyword activation.

- **H2 What Saucepan's importer reads** (`what-saucepans-importer-reads`)
  The importer takes PNG only and the chara keyword only, choosing V2 when data is present and V1 otherwise; no ccv3, JSON or charx. Default mapping, which the user can remap before saving: description personality and scenario merge into Information, creator_notes becomes the public Description, first_mes and alternate_greetings become Scenarios 1..N, mes_example becomes example dialogue, name and tags carry. System prompt, post-history instructions, character_book and extensions are dropped. The result is a private draft and oversized fields are clipped with a toast, not rejected.
- **H2 What the adapter writes** (`what-the-adapter-writes`)
  The adapter writes the normal V2 card from the Tavern codec via v2CardObject, embedded under chara alone by charaOnlyPng, carried by the inline PNG portrait or else the imported PNG; no field is rearranged because Saucepan's defaults already read V2 as authored, and a card with no PNG art is refused. saucepanWarnings reports each field the importer would clip, counted in code points: Information over 120,000, Description over 20,000, example dialogue over 40,000, any scenario over 30,000, or more than 50 greetings. The default map is a fixed table keyed by field name with no entry for system_prompt or post_history_instructions, but the mapping step offers Select mapping per discarded field and Formatting Instructions accepts them; when either prompt is non-empty the report says so and warns past the 10,000 limit.
- **H2 Lorebooks** (`lorebooks`)
  Saucepan lorebooks are chapters of title and text with no keyword activation. The Lorebook Importer takes any JSON and offers SillyTavern presets that map an entry's comment or keys to the chapter title and its content to the text; saucepan-lorebook writes ST world info for those presets and trigger keys survive only as text.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  The source-of-truth table names where each claim on this page lives. src/formats/saucepan/index.ts is the character adapter: it builds the V2 card, applies the Saucepan-specific handling, embeds the chara chunk, and attaches the serialize report with its warnings; it also default-exports the lorebook codec beside it. src/formats/saucepan/coverage.ts declares which canonical paths the Saucepan importer keeps, which drives the dropped list in every report and the export dialog's honesty lines. src/formats/_shared/card-png.ts holds v2CardObject and charaOnlyPng, shared with the other chara-only host, including the fallback from the portrait to the imported source PNG. src/formats/_shared/host-worldbook.ts wraps the SillyTavern worldbook codec under a host id. src/formats/saucepan/saucepan.test.ts pins the chunk keywords, field placement, refusals and report contents.

### SillyTavern format

- id: `reference/formats/sillytavern`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/sillytavern.md`
- SillyTavern format documents how its adapters detect, map, and round-trip Character Card V1 V2 and V3 cards plus standalone world info through the canonical model. Character JSON is the default export and PNG is available on request; V3 PNGs carry full V3 JSON in ccv3 and a V2-shaped chara backfill. One folder exports character, lorebook, regex, and persona codecs. Detection distinguishes character and lorebook paths; field mapping uses shared Tavern fields and lore enums. The original twin and sourceMedia support same-format field fidelity through a three-state overlay; JSON formatting and regenerated PNG chunks are not byte-identical promises. V3-only fields stay in ccv3 and the PNG backfill's extensions.vaudeville.escrow.fields; standalone V2 JSON has no CCv3-only slots. Other quirks include dual PNG chunks, talkativeness tolerance, world links by name, displayIndex, and regex and persona codecs. The folder also exports preset and prompt-format codecs: the preset codec reads KoboldAI, TextGen and NovelAI sampler sets, system prompts and reasoning templates besides chat-completion presets, and the prompt-format codec reads instruct and context templates. Its PNG sample is a synthetic card fixture with retained structure, not a real-corpus sample.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  Detection follows registry highest score above 0.5 so more specific formats outrank generic ones.
