Character detect returns 0.9 for a recognizable card and 0 otherwise, not 1.0, because SillyTavern is the generic Tavern reader and RoleCall claims 1.0 on CCv3 with extensions.rolecall. Two recognition paths: PNG with chara V2 or ccv3 V3 text chunks scores 0.9 and non-card PNG falls through to JSON; JSON detectCard accepts chara_card_v3 or v2 with data, or flat objects with name plus a real character signal for V1 flat. Flat v1 is the cross-kind firewall so a worldbook name object never false-positives as a character. Lorebook returns 0.9 for native top-level entries object keyed by string index with entry-shaped values, 0.85 for portable character_book with entries array, and 0 otherwise so native wins any tie.
  - **H3 Character** (`character`)
    detect returns 0.9 for a recognizable card and 0 otherwise. Score is 0.9 not 1.0 so RoleCall on extensions.rolecall wins when present; plain CCv3 without that block is read by sillytavern. PNG path scores 0.9 when bytes carry chara V2 or ccv3 V3 text chunks; a non-card PNG falls through because real JSON reads carry both bytes and text. JSON detectCard recognizes chara_card_v3 with data, chara_card_v2 with data, or flat with name plus V1 core fields description personality scenario first_mes mes_example or V2-era alternate_greetings system_prompt post_history_instructions creator_notes extensions character_version. V2-era fields mark variant flat otherwise v1; worldbooks carry none so they never score positive.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook`)
    sillytavern-lorebook returns 0.9 for a native worldbook, 0.85 for a portable character_book, and 0 otherwise. Score 0.9 requires top-level entries as an object keyed by string index not an array, which would be RoleCall or Agnai, whose first value looks like an entry via key keysecondary comment or content. Score 0.85 is a Chub-style or card-extracted character_book object with an entries array, portable as ST but scored below native so the native file wins any tie.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  Field map covers Character shared Tavern data then Lorebook enums and entry wires. Character data mapping lives in tavern-fields dataToBody and applyBodyToData shared with Risu: identity name nickname description characterVersion, persona personality scenario, greetings first alternate group-only, examples mes_example, prompts system post_history depth_prompt, attribution creator notes source multilingual dates, discovery tags, settings talkativeness, worldName, assets to media, character_book via bundle knowledgeRefs, and PNG pixels in original.sillytavern.sourceMedia. depth_prompt talkativeness and world are first-classed from extensions; empty depth prompt maps to no injection. Lorebook uses lore-enums for selectiveLogic role position integers; entries map comment content disable keys order group match toggles vectorized automationId displayIndex; embedded character_book uses CCv3 dialect with ST extensions bag.
  - **H3 Character** (`character-1`)
    Shared Tavern data mapping covers data.name nickname description character_version to identity, personality scenario to persona, first_mes alternate_greetings group_only_greetings to greetings, mes_example system_prompt post_history_instructions, extensions.depth_prompt as origin depth_prompt, creator creator_notes source creator_notes_multilingual dates, tags, extensions.talkativeness with tolerant string or number, extensions.world as worldName not embedded book, assets to portrait and media, and character_book via bundle extract into knowledgeRefs not tavern-fields. PNG carrier pixels live in original.sillytavern.sourceMedia. Empty depth_prompt.prompt maps to no injection so depth and role ride the twin; role is carried only when authored. Resolved empty knowledgeRefs removes both primary character_book and legacy extensions.character_book so clearing a link cannot resurrect the twin book.
  - **H3 Lorebook** (`lorebook-1`)
    ST world info encodes selectiveLogic role and position as integers via shared lore-enums: selectiveLogic 0-3 and_any through and_all, role 0-2 system user assistant, position 0-4 world character before_example after_example depth. Entry highlights map title from comment, content, enabled as not disable, triggers from key with inline regex decode, secondaryTriggers keysecondary, sortOrder from order, priority defaults 100 with no ST eviction axis, groupName group, scan match toggles including depth prompt and creator notes, vectorized groupOverride useGroupScoring automationId displayIndex. Embedded character_book uses CCv3 top-level keys secondary_keys insertion_order use_regex coarse before_char after_char with ST-extended fields in extensions, extensions winning when both present.
- **H2 Chats** (`chats`)
  Chats imports a SillyTavern chat .jsonl as a scene through the same import route as a card: inspect reads it, save-bundle or save-staged saves it into the ChatStore, and the Library drop sheet lists it. The codec returns a scene carrier of names, not a library entity, and is not in the adapter registry. Saving resolves the character by name and refuses the file, writing nothing, when the card is missing, ambiguous or already imported. mes is the shown text, swipes and swipe_id become the message swipes, times read in three shapes, and model, reasoning and generation time ride on each swipe. A user line matches its persona by avatar file, then by name. Hidden lines keep a hidden flag, chat variables become take variables, and group chats are refused whole.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  Escrow is the wrapper original Record keyed by format id; the character twin lives at original.sillytavern with the parsed card, unmapped variant v2 v3 v1 or flat, and sourceMedia b64 png only for PNG input. Same-format field fidelity comes from overlaying the body onto a clone of raw while preserving the original variant wrapper. Overlay is three-state against the twin decode via applyBodyToData: equal leaves parsed wire values untouched, changed writes, missing clears at the exact wire home; unknown keys and non-representable raw stay. Cross-format carries only the canonical body; raw extensions unknown keys and PNG stay in escrow uncopied. Lorebook collapses richer positions append append_bottom to depth 4, prepend_top to 0, scene to before_example 2; triggerMode always simple on read. Unedited entries re-project from raw twin by id preserving fields and raw-only carriers; JSON formatting may normalize. Raw-only uid vectors loreCache ride escrow untouched.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Quirks pin ST edges. V3-only fields stay in the full V3 envelope, and a V3 PNG's chara backfill escrows them under extensions.vaudeville.escrow.fields while ccv3 keeps the full card; media upgrades a non-V3 twin to V3. A V3 card writes both ccv3 and chara chunks, and the reader prefers ccv3.   World is by name, and displayIndex is never fabricated from sortOrder. The regex codec scores 0.9 for a bare array and 0.85 for a card block; the persona codec scores 0.95, imports the default persona and seals the whole backup; same-named personas get the avatar stem in the shown name only, and connections ride sealed. The preset codec also reads KoboldAI, TextGen and NovelAI sampler sets, system prompts and reasoning templates onto samplers, stock-ordered blocks and a reasoning field, sealing what Bunzai never sends, and an unedited import exports back to its file. The prompt-format codec reads instruct and context templates as one piece; a wrapper with a sysprompt stays a preset.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth maps concerns to files. Character adapter detect toCanonical fromCanonical is formats sillytavern index.ts. World info codec is lorebook.ts; regex and persona codecs are sibling regex.ts and persona.ts. Shared Tavern field map is tavern-fields.ts; lore int-enum decoders are lore-enums.ts; embedded character_book mapper is character-book.ts; CCv3 assets to media is assets.ts; PNG chunk read write is png.ts. Coverage declaration is coverage.ts. Canonical wrapper escrow original and id policy live in core canonical.ts. Samples under samples sillytavern include CCv2 and v3 JSON plus the synthetic-character.png structural fixture.

### vaud-json format

- id: `reference/formats/vaud-json`
- path: `docs/reference/formats/vaud-json.md`
- vaud-json is Bunzai's native character format: the canonical entity written directly to plain JSON with no wire translation. It serializes schemaVersion, kind, id, body, profiles, and original unchanged, making it the lossless local save and interchange format. The codec is native, so import and conversion use it while publish surfaces omit it. Detection parses through the exhaustive canonical runtime schema and scores 1.0 only for a valid character; invalid JSON, unsupported versions, malformed bodies, and other entity kinds score 0. Import uses the same parser and kind narrowing, eliminating a second partial validator. The field map is one-to-one, and export pretty-prints the whole entity. Existing escrow entries pass through untouched because vaud-json does not create its own twin. Quirks cover bounded parse errors, pure JSON media, and native exclusion from coverage. Source of truth lists the adapter, its tests, the shared runtime schema, registry, canonical wrapper, adapter contract, and UI publish filters.

- **H2 Detection** (`detection`)
  detect parses input through parseCanonicalEntity and returns 1.0 only when the complete result is a character. Missing text, invalid JSON, unsupported schema versions, malformed required or optional fields, unknown authored keys, and other entity kinds return 0. toCanonical uses the same exhaustive parser and then narrows to character, so detection and import no longer maintain separate validation rules or accept a fixture-shaped partial body.
- **H2 Field map** (`field-map`)
  There is no wire translation to tabulate. On-disk JSON is the canonical entity one to one: schemaVersion kind id body profiles and original map to themselves. Every CharacterBody field the eight always-present sub-objects identity persona prompts greetings examples media attribution discovery plus optional presentation settings bias worldName knowledgeRefs behavior behaviorRefs variants is written verbatim under body. The adapter has no opinion on field meanings; see entities character for semantics. fromCanonical is JSON.stringify entity null 2 with suggested extension json. It writes the whole entity including any original and profiles the entity carries with no filtering or re-projection.
- **H2 Escrow and round-trip** (`escrow-and-round-trip`)
  vaud-json is the one format where same-format round-trip is lossless exact and unconditional rather than reconstructed from an escrowed twin. Because it serializes the canonical entity directly, vaud-json to canonical to vaud-json returns the identical entity same body original and profiles untouched, proven by the adapter round-trip test and the registry-level round-trip test. Relationship to escrow differs from app adapters: those fill an original entry keyed by their own id on import so private fields survive same-format round-trip. vaud-json neither reads nor writes an original entry keyed to itself; it carries whatever original the entity already holds straight through because toCanonical and fromCanonical never inspect that field. A card imported from SillyTavern then saved as vaud-json keeps original.sillytavern.raw intact in the file ready to re-project if later exported back to SillyTavern.
- **H2 Quirks** (`quirks`)
  Detection and import both use the exhaustive canonical parser. Hand-edited files with unsupported schema versions, missing required sections, invalid optional fields, unknown authored keys, or another entity kind fail at the adapter boundary. Invalid JSON and invalid canonical character failures use separate bounded messages. Exports remain two-space pretty JSON. The format has no PNG or ZIP container; media stays in canonical refs or data URIs. native keeps the adapter off Press, export publish lists, and the platform coverage API while conversion and Workbench editing still use it.
- **H2 Source of truth** (`source-of-truth`)
  Source of truth maps the adapter to formats vaud-json index.ts, its regressions to vaud-json.test.ts, and exhaustive parsing to entities/runtime-schema.ts. Registry selection lives in core/registry.ts; canonical wrapper and escrow in core/canonical.ts; adapter flags in core/adapter.ts; and character fields in entities/character/schema.ts. Press, export dialog, and setup publish filters exclude native adapters. FORMAT-SUPPORT.md carries the generated matrix row.

## reference/kit

### Kit reference

- id: `reference/kit`
- path: `docs/reference/kit/README.md`
- Kit reference is the short index for Bunzai's conversational terminal surface. Kit uses the same canonical entities and Studio store as the Web UI, so terminal and browser share one model of truth. Content tools documents the capability catalog, progressive model exposure, and the implemented canonical content bundles. Kit can search and read catalog-declared Bunzai user and developer documentation through the read-only docs_query model tool without arbitrary filesystem paths. UI reference covers terminal shell behavior users can run today. Architecture documents semantic content capability ownership and dependency direction between entity pure layers and Kit runtime adapters.


### Kit content tools

- id: `reference/kit/tools`
- path: `docs/reference/kit/tools.md`
- Kit content tools separates model-facing runtime tools from terminal slash commands. Its lifecycle covers the eight listed lifecycle kinds through pure previews, one four-domain discovery catalog, complete draft evidence, guarded semantic review, documentation query, whole-piece reads, and bounded result handles. Twenty-six direct tools start a turn, or twenty-five without an engine checkout; search reveals up to five deferred schemas, browse reveals none, and exact describe reveals one. Typed creation covers character, lorebook, persona, preset, regex, pack, quick-reply set, and HTML document without an existing target. Duplicate retains escrow, delete is explicitly classified, and transfer previews adapter costs without writing. External MCP tools join the same exact-name egress gate and bounded result store, and connection changes invalidate the cached session. Guarded apply uses the application review card; atomic create-only apply refuses occupied IDs and verifies storage. Unit and integration proof exists, while a real-provider terminal run remains outstanding.

- **H2 Built lifecycle** (`built-lifecycle`)
  Built lifecycle connects the eight listed lifecycle kinds to Kit. Entity capability folders own stable IDs, search, exposure, and pure semantic operations; Kit discovers and adapts them into one registry. Eight typed create workflows draft without writing: character, lorebook, persona, preset, regex, pack, quick-reply set, and HTML document. Duplicate carries body, profiles, and escrow. studio_delete alone destroys canonical content and remains direct because deferred apply workflows are rejected. studio_transfer previews another adapter without writing. Preset verification, regex diagnostics, drawing-to-regex transcription, shared-folder search and import, rail opening, whole-piece reads, and HTML document update complete the live lifecycle. The change session retains one baseline, composed operations, warnings, platform impact, and escrow until a separately authorized apply. studio_export writes text and binary artifacts create-only into fresh timestamped Export folders through the shared synced write owner.
- **H2 Capability contract** (`capability-contract`)
  A content capability is a pure semantic operation declaring a stable dotted ID, kind, navigation metadata, platform applicability, Zod input, effect, exposure, concurrency key, and preview. Preview returns a complete canonical entity, changes, warnings, platform impact, and optionally a deterministic read observation. It never writes. The composer rejects identity or original escrow changes. The read adapter also requires a canonically identical entity and empty changes, preventing analysis from creating a mutation draft.
- **H2 Apply and authorization** (`apply-and-authorization`)
  The safety subsystem owns exact-name access classification and never trusts prefixes, discovery metadata, or a claimed runtime effect. Pure content capabilities derive read or preview-only draft access from validated catalog metadata. Each of the eight creation workflows has a separate safety-owned exact-name draft classification. Guarded apply pauses in an application-owned semantic review card. Update apply compares the expected revision under a per-path lock. Create apply uses a distinct atomic create-only operation that refuses an occupied ID rather than overwriting or minting a sibling. Stale state and collisions write nothing. Both modes re-read and verify canonical fields and non-Studio escrow. Unreadable or mismatched verification reports failure without retry.
- **H2 Progressive exposure** (`progressive-exposure`)
  Progressive exposure keeps a large catalog out of every prompt. The runtime registry holds every adapted capability and deferred workflow so dispatch can resolve a selection, and twenty-six direct tools begin each turn, among them html_will_draw, regex_from_drawing, regex_lab, studio_delete and studio_export; preset_verify is the one conditional entry, taking itself out where no engine checkout exists. Delete and export are direct for a structural reason: the deferred path classifies a tool from discovery metadata and the runtime refuses to let that path describe an apply effect, so a destructive or file-writing workflow cannot be discovered into existence and must be granted by exact name in the trust map. Routing removal through draft and apply would need ChangeDraftMode to grow a remove arm, deferred to its own pass. One catalog accepts content, studio, transfer, and diagnostics domains; transfer is populated by studio_transfer while diagnostics remains an empty slot. Only pure-content capabilities derive safe access from catalog metadata. Search reveals at most five ranked matches, browse none, describe one. The deferred set clears each turn while drafts persist.
  - **H3 Canonical reads and result handles** (`canonical-reads-and-result-handles`)
    studio_read defaults to one complete canonical entity. A normal piece up to 64,000 characters returns inline in one observation, avoiding forced section traversal. It can still outline immediate children as RFC 6901 JSON Pointers or read one selected value with offsets and limits. Invalid pointers fail closed. Larger whole pieces enter a session-local store and return an opaque result-N handle plus a 4,096-character peek. Other result-producing tools retain the shared 4,096-character inline threshold. result_query provides stat, bounded read, and literal case-insensitive search. Handles reveal no path and expire with the session. Storage caps are 24 entries, 1,000,000 UTF-8 bytes per entry, and 4,000,000 UTF-8 bytes total with oldest-first eviction. Content beyond the store cap remains recoverable through direct studio_read offsets.
  - **H3 Complete draft review** (`complete-draft-review`)
    Every composed operation retains validated input, changes, warnings, and platform impact, while the draft aggregates warning and impact evidence over one immutable baseline. change_query list returns compact state and counts. show returns operations, baseline, complete proposed entity, warnings, and platform impact, using a result handle or explicit offsets for large proposals. validate re-parses canonical shape and compares the live stored revision without mutating draft state or writing. Per-operation rollback remains excluded until deterministic replay is specified.
- **H2 Character slice** (`character-slice`)
  studio.character.create needs no existing target. It accepts a safe optional ID or derives one, then builds a canonical character from identity, persona, scenario, greeting, examples, tags, portrait, and additional media. Portrait and media schemas describe data URI, HTTP(S), and existing asset references, MIME types, roles, labels, and primary selection. It returns structured application-Gate review and uses atomic create-only verified apply. Existing characters retain ten preview-only capabilities covering identity, prompts, greetings, metadata, presentation, media, links, variants, settings, and embedded behavior scripts. Script payloads remain sealed data and original escrow remains non-editable.
- **H2 Lorebook slice** (`lorebook-slice`)
  studio.lorebook.create builds a new book and optionally typed initial entries with content, literal or regex triggers, selective logic, insertion position, depth, role, and defaults for the remaining canonical activation fields. Existing drop-ins are settings.update, entries.update, entries.reorder, entries.enable, and entries.remove. Duplicate, separate entry-create, trigger-specific update, placement-specific update, and broad bulk operations remain future additions. The Web UI retains local selection, focus, generated-ID, and undo behavior.
- **H2 Other canonical bundles** (`other-canonical-bundles`)
  Persona creation separates shelf brief from injected content and accepts sections, traits, identity, rating, and portrait. Preset creation accepts prompt blocks, samplers, and media settings. Regex creation accepts stored rules but never executes them. Pack creation accepts expression items and groups. Quick-reply creation accepts labeled messages, and Workbench owns later editing. HTML-document creation stores sealed markup, while htmldoc.document.update edits an existing drawing. Existing semantic capabilities edit supported kinds through shared entity reducers.
- **H2 Macro translation across engines** (`macro-translation-across-engines`)
  Macro translation across engines states that a preset crossing to another engine is translated rather than only reported on, because refusing over one imperfect token leaves the job unfinished and authored prose outweighs a macro that was never going to fire. The engine computes every equivalence and the model explains it, never the reverse, since a model reasoning from names alone is confidently wrong when names collide but meanings do not follow them. There is no engine-pair table: project rule 1 forbids format-to-format conversion for macros as it does for codecs, because five engines would mean twenty directed pairs. Each catalog instead declares what its own macros mean through canonical operations, and every cross-engine answer is a join on that meaning. Crossings produce portable, collision, absent or flatten verdicts. A gate asserts no shared name diverges in shape without a declared operation. Risu, Agnai and unverified SillyTavern annotations are named as honest limits.
  - **H3 Hub and spoke, the same as formats** (`hub-and-spoke-the-same-as-formats`)
    Hub and spoke explains why macros have no engine-pair table. Project rule 1 forbids format-to-format conversion for macros as it does for codecs, because five engines would mean twenty directed pairs and each new engine would multiply the work. Each catalog instead declares what its own macros mean through canonical operations, and every cross-engine answer is a join on that meaning. Adding an engine is one annotation pass. Operations attach per entry rather than per name, because one name can mean different things at different argument counts: RoleCall reads random as a range at two arguments and a pick at three or more. Annotation is partial by design, since unannotated entries fall back to name matching.
  - **H3 What a crossing produces** (`what-a-crossing-produces`)
    A crossing produces one of four verdicts. Portable means both engines perform the operation and the token is re-spelled in the target's punctuation. Collision means the same name performs a different operation, so it is left as authored and flagged rather than silently kept. Absent means the target has no macro for the operation, so it is removed with same-family candidates. Flatten means a block the target cannot express, where the body is kept and only the scaffolding removed. Random with two arguments crossing RoleCall to SillyTavern is the worked example: a range meets a pick, and the collision is derived rather than listed anywhere.
  - **H3 The gate that keeps it honest** (`the-gate-that-keeps-it-honest`)
    The gate is a function returning any macro name that several engines share with differing argument shapes where at least one side has not declared an operation, paired with a test asserting that list stays empty. This is what keeps the hub trustworthy as engines are added: a shared name spelled differently, whose meaning nobody has written down, is exactly where a silent mistranslation hides. Because divergence is detected mechanically rather than by reading, the system reports which annotations are owed instead of leaving a person to hunt for them, and a contributor cannot introduce a silent mistranslation by adding an engine and forgetting to declare what one of its macros means.
  - **H3 Honest limits** (`honest-limits`)
    Honest limits names what the translation layer cannot currently prove. Risu with its bracket CBS dialect and Agnai with its named-slot templates are not modeled at all, so an unmodeled target reports unchecked, which a caller must render as unknown and never as clean. SillyTavern's catalog is the only one with no oracle to verify against, so its annotations are the least grounded of the four, while RoleCall, Lumiverse and Marinara are machine-checked against their real registries by the macro oracle. Flag-prefixed tokens resolve to no name and are therefore never flagged, because the same prefix character means different things on different engines and stripping it would trade a false negative for a false positive.
- **H2 Bunzai documentation query** (`bunzai-documentation-query`)
  docs_query is a direct read-only meta-tool with four actions. browse lists collections and pages from index metadata only, with root or immediate children, limit 12 cap 25, offset pagination, and slash-separated collection ids that never path-escape. outline returns a semantic page overview and collapsed H2 map without loading Markdown; a returned section slug expands only that branch and its H3 summaries. search ranks body text plus title, short summary, semantic summary, topics, and headings with in-memory BM25. read alone returns authoritative source prose: omit the section for a whole document or select a slug for a targeted nugget. Bounded responses provide continuation offsets so long documents can be read completely across calls. Whole-document reads are preferred around located answers to preserve qualifications, failure conditions, and current-versus-planned distinctions. Semantic navigation merges only from docs/summaries sidecars with current independent APPROVE receipts bound to source and semantic-summary hashes. Authoring discovery is folder-derived from docs/. New and edited pages require authoring, stamping, focused checking, and independent review. Global check requires every page APPROVED. No embeddings or network.
- **H2 Serving the belt to another client** (`serving-the-belt-to-another-client`)
  The bunzai mcp subcommand serves discovered tools and their schemas as newline-delimited JSON-RPC over stdio, without requiring source paths in an installed binary. A client configured by the person owns its own permission interface. The optional Claude subscription provider instead supplies only its Bunzai bridge: built-in tools and skills are explicitly empty, strict MCP configuration excludes inherited servers, and dontAsk denies other unapproved calls. The allowedTools setting grants permission rather than restricting availability. That bridge is read-only unless the person explicitly enables writes, with filtering enforced by the dispatcher so excluded tool names cannot execute. Bunzai implements its own small stdio protocol; the optional Claude SDK does have an MCP SDK peer pinned to a patched version because a transitive override did not update it. Isolated configuration tests establish these properties, without claiming real SDK execution proves complete isolation.
- **H2 Wearing other people's belts** (`wearing-other-peoples-belts`)
  MCP servers configured in Settings Connections join Kit's belt. The host-only API writes fail-closed machine-local mcp.json entries; environment values never return, and omitting env preserves stored values. Saving, disabling, or removing a server synchronizes the shared connection manager and invalidates the cached Kit session, so the next turn sees the exact new belt without restarting. One manager per process reuses enabled servers, restarts changed recipes, and kills children on exit. External names and schemas come from the server, collisions are dropped, and every enumerated name receives exact egress classification in the same assembly step. Unknown names remain denied, external tools cannot gain write, delete, or exec classification, and Bunzai does not re-export them. Large external results use the shared bounded store, returning a handle and peek or a named omission beyond its cap.
- **H2 Verification status** (`verification-status`)
  Unit and integration tests cover catalog parity, discovery for all eight create workflows, whole-card reads, bounded first-party and MCP results, draft evidence, stale and collision refusal, verified apply, and a fake-provider rename through the real store. Lifecycle tests cover delete, escrow-preserving duplicate, export containment, and a real-filesystem preset transfer. Macro catalogs are checked against available engine registries; SillyTavern lacks an oracle. A running Kit session against a real provider remains outstanding.

## reference/platforms/marinara

### Marinara agents, tools, and regex

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/agents-and-tools`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/agents-and-tools.md`
- Documents Marinara agents, Memory Recall and summaries, custom Functions, regex scripts, Professor Mari, and the map boundary at the pinned Engine docs for Kit retrieval. Agents cover activation, phases, abilities, result types, tools, and dynamic package inventory. Memory distinguishes semantic message chunks from Roleplay and Conversation summarization. Functions cover names, parameters, Static Result, Webhook, Script, hidden context, enable lists, import and export, and server restrictions. Regex covers placement, mode, scope, order, depth, import, and safety blocks. Professor Mari covers supported creation and editing, guided chips, selected connections, attachments, Skills, separate history, reversible saved-data edits, and its powerful install-folder command boundary. Hierarchical map semantics route to the dedicated page. Bunzai imports codecs and sealed data without executing Marinara assistants, tools, scripts, or maps.

- **H2 Agents** (`agents`)
  Agents are per-chat helpers enabled in Chat Settings. Official packages are mode-limited while custom agents run in all modes. Copy official packages to edit rather than mutating stock. Import export packages where documented. Phases are Pre-Generation Parallel and Post-Processing and result types may force phase. Post may optionally read Turn Data Access of pre-generation injections and parallel results. Abilities opt-in include create edit lorebooks target one book edit messages trackers frontend styling image generation vectors embeddings and main prompt edits. Result types include Context Injection Text Rewrite Lorebook Update Character Tracker Persona Stats Custom Tracker Game State Image Prompt Prompt Patch Frontend Style. Activation keywords skip irrelevant turns. Attach custom tools. Built-in inventory is dynamic from built-in agents docs and Marinara-Agents repo so static Bunzai pages must not freeze the list.
- **H2 Memory systems (two)** (`memory-systems-two`)
  Memory systems two are distinct product features not short-lived agent notes. Memory Recall works in all modes and is the only memory system in Game with no summaries. It runs semantic search over past messages with per-chat enable defaults on in Conversation and on in Roleplay or Game with active Scene else off. Embedding source is on the connection or local sidecar. Memories for This Chat modal shows Vectorized Waiting or Embedding unavailable chunks with export marinara json import merge rebuild clear and per-chunk forget. Chunks need at least five new messages similarity may return none and rebuild after model change. Storage can continue when the toggle is off if embedding is available. Lite may hide Memory Recall. Summaries are Roleplay Chat Summary compressing old messages and Conversation Automatic Summarization. Different jobs that can combine with Memory Recall.
- **H2 Custom tools (Functions)** (`custom-tools-functions`)
  Custom tools Functions live under Presets Functions. Name is lowercase snake_case one to one hundred characters description one to five hundred for the model unique with reserved built-in names blocked. Admin secret required for remote manage. Export ZIP import ZIP or JSON. Parameters are name type string number boolean array object Required description empty names dropped and invalid imported params can skip the tool at generation with server log only. Execution types exact are Static Result fixed string empty becomes OK Webhook POST JSON tool and arguments to URL reply at most five hundred twelve KB default sixty seconds https only private localhost blocked unless admin allows failures return error result and Script server JS with args JSON Math Date must return off by default until CUSTOM_TOOL_SCRIPT_ENABLED true greyed when disabled. Include hidden chat context default off for Webhook and Script adds mode persona characters chat variables game state without AI-passed args. Chat Settings Enable Tool Use off default. No explicit list means all globally enabled tools adding any tool restricts to that set.
- **H2 Regex scripts** (`regex-scripts`)
  Regex scripts live under Presets Regexes. Find without slashes Replace with dollar captures and case transforms flags g i m s u y d Trim Strings after replace Live Test pattern only not placement enable scope or depth. Macros allowed in find replace trim. Apply To AI Output and or User Input at least one. Apply Mode Only Display default Only Prompt or Both. User Input plus Only Display or Both rewrites the saved or sent message with no pure display-only for outbound user text. Execution Order lower first default auto-assigned. Depth Min Max from newest zero empty means any min greater than max blocks save. Specific Characters toggle or character Advanced Regex Scripts after save. Per-chat Scoped Regex Scripts Disabled default Exclusive or Chat with per-script toggles. Prompt-side scripts follow generating character. ST card import Character only or Global empty or unsafe patterns skipped. Safety blocks nested quantifiers ambiguous quantified alternatives oversized patterns over one thousand chars and multi broad wildcards slow run skips that message only.
- **H2 Professor Mari** (`professor-mari`)
  Professor Mari is Marinara's Home-screen assistant for explanation, agent-catalog comparison, and creation or editing of characters, personas, lorebooks, themes, agents, prompt presets, avatars, sprites, and backgrounds. Guided chips ask one focused question and fill editable drafts. Existing-data edits save then show a ten-minute Review Mari's changes card: Keep confirms and Restore reinstates the previous snapshot; new items usually skip it, and character or persona history is a second recovery path. It remembers a selected text connection, opens Connections when absent, accepts images PDFs and common text up to 20 MB, and needs vision for image reading. Custom Skills have upload, edit, enable, and delete lifecycle. Restart archives separate Mari chat history; Stop cancels work. Its repair authority may inspect or modify only the install folder and run short-lived commands there, never directly write saved data through that path. Remote edits require remote access. Bunzai does not import that command ability.
- **H2 Maps** (`maps`)
  The dedicated Maps and game content page owns the full Hierarchical Maps authoring, draft, enable, save, exact-location lore, one-move travel, import, archive, and Game-binding lifecycle. Agent Game State and tracker result types cannot bypass the map's current-location or save rules.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai currently provides Marinara regex and persona codecs for this cluster. Imported scripts remain sealed data. Bunzai does not run Marinara custom Functions, agent pipelines, Professor Mari install commands, or map updates during import or conversion. Unsupported behavior stays platform-owned and must not be inferred from documentation coverage.

### Marinara characters and personas

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/characters`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/characters.md`
- Documents Marinara character and persona authoring at the pinned Engine revision. Character creation and editing covers required name, optional avatar, Metadata, Card, Convo, Lorebook, Sprites, Gallery, Colors, Stats, Advanced, save states, version history, and reversible agent Keep or Restore updates. Convo semantics include display name and declare-on-card behavior, About Me defaults and chat-specific overrides, update_about_me Public versus Chat scopes, Convo Behavior insertion modes, and Conversation-only macros. Personas cover library filtering, one active global persona, per-chat override precedence, equivalent editor tabs, and user-name fallback. Import and export distinguishes JSON CCv2, embedded PNG, charx, richest Marinara native packages, batch tag and regex choices, embedded-lorebook promotion, compatible loss, and native-only bulk ZIP. Bunzai has no dedicated Marinara character codec; adapter claims and sprite or gallery fidelity must come from implemented format support.

- **H2 Character create and editor** (`character-create-and-editor`)
  Character create and editor requires Name and optional avatar with tabs Metadata Card Convo Lorebook Sprites Gallery Colors Stats Advanced. Unsaved leave offers Keep editing Discard or Save and close. Save disabled until dirty with Uploading Embedding Saving states. Header Favorite Export Import as persona Duplicate Delete. Metadata child covers identity talkativeness tags Creator Notes never AI version history avatar crop. Card child holds permanent prose greetings and examples. Convo tab Conversation Mode only child documents Convo Display Name declare-on-card About Me auto-list and chat override popout update_about_me Public versus Chat Convo Behavior insertion modes Constant after before Append Prepend Replace post-history or Only where convo_behavior is placed and Conversation-only macros without deferring required semantics to unclassified upstream pages. Advanced child System Prompt PHI Depth Prompt mode apply flags. Version history and agent updates Keep Restore.
  - **H3 Metadata** (`metadata`)
    Metadata includes Character ID copyable after save, Name for the char macro, Phonetic name for TTS and macros, Creator, Version, Talkativeness 0-100 percent default 50 for group speak rate, Tags with comma multi-add and remove all, Creator Notes never sent to the AI, Version history panel, and avatar crop.
  - **H3 Card** (`card`)
    Card holds Description, Personality, Backstory, Appearance which also seeds the AI avatar prompt, Scenario, First Message, Alternate Greetings with reorder and remove, and Example Dialogue using START blocks plus user and char labels.
  - **H3 Convo tab (Conversation Mode only)** (`convo-tab-conversation-mode-only`)
    Convo tab Conversation Mode only is never used in Roleplay or Game. Convo Display Name blanks use card name and update messages immediately; characters may declare the name on the card with a mapping line. About Me auto-lists into Conversation prompts with Professor Mari Keep Restore and chat-specific override popout Default versus Chat-specific badges plus status. update_about_me needs Tool Use with Public global approval versus Chat private scope. Convo Behavior insertion modes Constant after or before card Append Prepend Replace post-history or Only where convo_behavior is placed. Macros convo_display char_about persona_about convo_behavior. Auto list and Behavior run without macros.
  - **H3 Advanced** (`advanced`)
    Advanced holds System Prompt which does not replace the main system prompt and uses mode-specific insertion, Post-History Instructions near generation, and Depth Prompt default depth 4 with roles System User Assistant. Apply flags cover Conversation, Roleplay, VN, and Game; presets do not disable PHI or Depth.
  - **H3 Version history and agent updates** (`version-history-and-agent-updates`)
    Version history and agent updates stores prior saves for restore. Agents including Professor Mari may propose card updates that apply then expose Keep or Restore review. Treat the flow as a reversible product workflow, never as silent irreversible overwrite of author content.
- **H2 Personas** (`personas`)
  Personas panel is a full library with New, Import, Select bulk, search, sort A-Z Z-A Newest Oldest Tokens, folders, All Active Inactive and tags filters. At most one active global persona; new import and duplicate never auto-activate. Chat can override persona separately per choosing-your-persona. Persona editor tabs are Metadata with Persona ID crop Name Creator Phonetic Title Comment Version default 1.0 Tags Creator Notes Version history, Card with the same five prose fields and macros, Convo, Lorebook, Sprites, Gallery, Colors, Stats. Header offers Export persona, Add persona as character, Duplicate, Delete. Unsaved leave banner matches characters. The user macro uses chat persona if set else active persona else generic User with no persona details.
- **H2 Import / export** (`import-export`)
  Import / export supports formats json CCv2, png embedded, charx CCv3 zip, and marinara or marinara.json richest. Batch drop is supported. Whole-batch import options are Tags All default, No tags, or Existing only; Regex Character only default or Global Presets then Regexes; Embedded lorebook Import Lorebook as standalone linked or No Import keep only in card. Single export choices are Marinara Native with metadata sprites gallery attached lorebooks, Compatible JSON CCv2, and Compatible PNG; Compatible drops Marinara-only extras. Bulk select exports a zip of Native only named marinara-characters.zip. ST folder import is Settings Imports documented under data-and-interop.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai has no dedicated marinara character codec; Tavern-shaped cards often use SillyTavern adapters. Persona and regex Marinara codecs exist. Do not invent sprite or gallery fidelity on compatible PNG export. Report losses honestly when Marinara-only assets leave the native package format.

### Marinara chats and group chats

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/chats-and-interchange`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/chats-and-interchange.md`
- Documents Marinara chat interchange, group behavior, connected-chat relationships, and per-chat setting ownership. JSONL is re-importable while Text is not; single export offers either, bulk export is JSONL-only, new-chat import takes the open mode tab, and branch import reuses the open chat's characters, persona, connection, and prompt preset. Hidden reasoning is excluded from all exports by default. Group chats form at two characters: Roleplay provides Merged or Individual generation with Sequential, Smart, or Manual order, while Conversation provides Reply When Mentioned and Character Exchanges. Disabling every member reactivates all as a safety fallback. Connected Chats is a one-to-one asymmetric Conversation-to-story link, distinct from automatic Cross-Chat Awareness, with influence, durable note, and ooc transfer tags plus disconnect cleanup. Chat Settings and reusable Chat Settings Presets own bounded per-chat configuration without rewriting library artifacts. Bunzai requires an implemented adapter before claiming chat conversion.

- **H2 File formats** (`file-formats`)
  File formats state that JSONL is the default re-importable chat form accepted for Marinara and SillyTavern JSONL interchange, while Text txt is a human-readable transcript that cannot be re-imported. There is no separate undocumented Marinara-native chat container beyond JSONL and Text as documented. Kit must not invent binary or proprietary chat carriers when advising export paths.
- **H2 Single export** (`single-export`)
  Single export uses the open chat Switch branch panel to download JSONL or Text for the open chat including its messages. Authors choose format per download. This per-chat export surface is separate from the mode-tab bulk selection path and ZIP workflow.
- **H2 Bulk export** (`bulk-export`)
  Bulk export runs from the mode tab CONVO RP or GM after selecting chats then Export. The result is a zip of JSONL only with one file per selected chat of that mode. Text bulk export is not this path. Mode membership of the selection follows the open tab not a mode field inside each file itself.
- **H2 Import as new chat** (`import-as-new-chat`)
  Import as new chat uses the chat list import control whose tooltip names SillyTavern or Marinara chat JSONL. Import creates a new chat in the currently open mode tab CONVO RP or GM rather than reading a mode encoded in the file. Success reports imported message count and switches into the chat. Wrong open tab yields the wrong mode for the new chat container.
- **H2 Import as branch** (`import-as-branch`)
  Import as branch uses Branch panel Import of JSONL to create a new branch of the open chat. The branch reuses that chat characters persona connection and prompt preset rather than inventing new membership from the file alone. Use this path when continuing an existing chat container rather than starting a new mode-tab chat session.
- **H2 Reasoning in exports** (`reasoning-in-exports`)
  Reasoning in exports is controlled by Settings Advanced Message Tools Include reasoning in exports which defaults off. Off strips hidden thinking from both JSONL and Text for single and bulk exports. On includes reasoning; leave the toggle off before sharing exports that must not leak chain-of-thought content.
- **H2 Group chats** (`group-chats`)
  Group chats form when two or more characters are present without a separate group button. Conversation and Roleplay support them; Game uses its party system. Creation may select several characters, add a folder roster, or use Dice pick. Chat Settings supports add, remove, drag reorder, and per-chat eye-disable; disabling every character re-enables all as a zero-speaker fallback. Roleplay provides Merged Narrator or Individual with Sequential, Smart, and Manual response orders, Color Dialogues, Add Turn To Prompt, Name Prefix History, and chat-local Scenario Override. Conversation instead defaults to multi-character replies and provides Reply When Mentioned plus Character Exchanges under Autonomous Messaging.
  - **H3 Roleplay group settings** (`roleplay-group-settings`)
    Roleplay group settings choose Merged Narrator default versus Individual. Merged optional Color Dialogues uses character Colors tab. Individual Response Order is Sequential list order Smart hidden AI pick with mention override and possible multi-queue or Manual Trigger Response only. Individual toggles Add Turn To Prompt default on and Name Prefix History default off. Scenario Override can replace card scenarios when present.
  - **H3 Conversation group speaking** (`conversation-group-speaking`)
    Conversation group speaking does not use Roleplay's Group Chat panel. Default replies may voice several characters with automatic speaker coloring. Reply When Mentioned makes members wait for an at-name mention or manual Trigger Response selection. Character Exchanges, off by default, allows members to talk to one another only while the browser app remains open and consumes the autonomous daily-message allowance. Schedules, presence, and per-chat member disable state further constrain participation without creating Sequential, Smart, or Manual Roleplay order.
- **H2 Connected chats** (`connected-chats`)
  Connected Chats is a deliberate one-to-one link between one Conversation and one Roleplay or Game chat. Conversation automatically reads recent story messages; the story side does not automatically read Conversation. Character-emitted influence sends one-turn steering, note stores durable story-side prompt notes, and ooc posts Roleplay text back to Conversation. This differs from default-on Cross-Chat Awareness among sibling Conversations sharing a character. Links start from Conversation or Game, exclude already-linked chats, add bidirectional toolbar switching, and disconnect or chat deletion clears pending influences and saved notes. Story Chat Settings provides per-note deletion and confirmed Clear all.
- **H2 Chat settings ownership** (`chat-settings-ownership`)
  Chat settings ownership places lorebook pins, agent enables, budgets, persona override, group modes, scoped regex, tool use, autonomous messaging, and Advanced Parameters on one chat. Reusable Chat Settings Presets may capture connection, prompt preset, agents, tools, translation, memory, and generation overrides, but omit characters, persona, lorebooks, sprites, summary, tags, scene prompt, and Roleplay Scenario Override. Library cards and prompt presets remain separate; the built-in Default preset cannot be renamed, overwritten, or deleted.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai does not convert Marinara chat JSONL unless an adapter exists. Kit must not invent chat codecs from this interchange page. Export privacy and mode-tab import rules describe upstream product behavior, not proven canonical interchange or round-trip support inside Bunzai.

### Marinara Conversation Mode authoring

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/conversation-mode`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/conversation-mode.md`
- Complete Marinara Conversation Mode authoring reference at the pinned Engine revision. Profiles route to Convo Display Name, About Me defaults and chat overrides, update_about_me scopes, Behavior insertion modes, and Conversation-only macros. The four-step new-chat wizard owns name and connection, prompt preset, participants, and Automation with autonomous messages, schedules, and package-contributed command families. Schedules include chat and character caps, tuning, timing, daily blocks, AI draft actions, temporary status, and user presence. Custom media covers portable emoji export, sticker send modes, Giphy limits, gallery scope, and semantic/random/tool-call selection. Calls document required audio pipeline, four input modes, per-chat versus global settings, answer lifecycle, soundboard, reusable video clips, and history summary. Selfies define every chat setting and reviewed manual generation. Six table games include install, start paths, participant limits, defaults, enforced state, autonomous invitation gates, and early-end deletion. These are upstream product surfaces, not automatic Bunzai adapter capabilities.

- **H2 Profiles (Display Name, About Me, Behavior)** (`profiles-display-name-about-me-behavior`)
  Profiles Display Name About Me Behavior defers full field semantics to Characters Convo tab Conversation Mode only. That destination owns Convo Display Name with declare-on-card checkbox for characters, About Me auto-list defaults and per-chat override popout badges, update_about_me Public versus Chat scopes with Tool Use, Convo Behavior insertion modes Constant after or before card Append Prepend Replace post-history or Only where convo_behavior is placed, and Conversation-only macros convo_display char_about persona_about convo_behavior. Auto About listing and Behavior insertion run without macros; macros place values in custom prompts. Fields never apply in Roleplay or Game.
- **H2 Getting started boundary** (`getting-started-boundary`)
  Getting started defines Conversation as messenger-style continuing relationship state whose profiles, presence, autonomous messages, schedules, reactions, selfies, calls, and table games do not apply in Roleplay or Game. Its four-step wizard assigns Name and Connection, Prompt Preset, Persona and Characters, then Automation. Autonomous Messages starts on and Generate Schedules off. Optional packages add a master Commands gate plus up to seventeen families for schedule updates, cross-post, selfies, memories, scenes, music, haptics, connected-chat influence or notes, calls, reactions, and six games. User presence is global Active, Idle, Do Not Disturb, or Invisible with a 120-character activity; DND suppresses autonomous messages while Idle does not.
- **H2 Schedules and autonomous messaging** (`schedules-and-autonomous-messaging`)
  Schedules and autonomous messaging live in Conversation Chat Settings. Autonomous Messages defaults on. Chat Check-In Cap uses talkativeness ceilings of eight, six, five, three, or two daily, or a positive whole-number chat ceiling; character limits only lower it. Schedules default off and provide generated weekly routines, per-character day editors, and stale-aware routine summaries. Tuning selects five talkativeness levels, 15-360 minute wait with 120 default, and five enabled check-in moments. Advanced timing sets Default or 1-8 daily safety, optional Away 0-120 delay with random 1-3 blank default, and Busy 0-120 with random 2-5. Rewrite, Adjust, Vary, or Repair changes a draft only until Save. Daily Online/Away/Busy/Offline blocks affect initiation; temporary status does not rewrite the schedule. Manual user presence disables automatic Idle until Active returns.
- **H2 Custom emojis, stickers, and GIFs** (`custom-emojis-stickers-and-gifs`)
  Custom emojis, stickers, and GIFs are Conversation-only beyond the plain emoji picker. Emoji names are one to thirty-two lowercase letters, numbers, or underscores, unique, at most 256 by 256, and may be animated GIFs. Edit mode renames, deletes, exports a self-contained marinara-custom-emojis JSON, and imports while skipping invalid or colliding items; deleted shortcodes remain text in history. Stickers use a parallel lifecycle with 512 by 512 limit and Send and reply versus Add to message. Giphy search needs a key, has fixed mature rating, and no offline safe-only source. Gallery assets can receive profile-scoped tags without copying. Per-chat selection is Semantic with local/random fallback, Random, or Tool-call with semantic fallback and multi-speaker skip; Max offered defaults twenty in range one to one hundred. Reactions use only global emoji.
- **H2 Calls and clips** (`calls-and-clips`)
  Calls require the optional package, per-chat Audio/Video Calls, and global Call Audio Pipeline even for typed use; TTS is optional and unresolved voices become text-only. Input modes are Local Whisper recording default, browser speech with fallback, manual dictation, and provider-native audio/video, the only mode allowing camera or screen. Per-chat defaults are calls off, character Calls command on, and voice cues on; pipeline, camera, video presence, automatic clips, and custom clips are global and off. Incoming calls always need Answer or Decline; active calls persist in a popout. Soundboard has four immutable built-ins plus mp3/wav/ogg/webm/m4a uploads to 8 MB. Video Presence uses per-character Idle, Talking, Laughing, Angry, Crying, and Sighing Clips, distinct from Gallery Videos; automation creates only missing Idle and Talking. Disabling video presence disables its two child toggles. Call-end summary enters chat history.
- **H2 Selfies** (`selfies`)
  Selfies are Conversation-only Illustrator image events. Commands and Generated Selfies must be enabled. Per-chat settings are required Selfie Connection initially None, Prompt Model default Main chat model, Image Style default global profile, Send Avatar References off and provider-dependent, Attach Card Appearance off, and Resolution default 896x1152 with five alternate presets. A character may invoke the hidden command, while the user can request from Gallery and choose a group member. Expose media prompts before sending allows review and editing; Cancel sends no image request. Each completed selfie consumes one selected-connection request and is not a Roleplay scene background.
- **H2 Table games** (`table-games`)
  Table games are six optional Conversation packages: UNO, Chess, Poker, 8-Ball Pool, Tic-Tac-Toe, and Rock-Paper-Scissors. Installation immediately exposes slash, chat-phrase, and separately gated character-invitation paths. One game may exist per chat and at least one character is required. UNO seats two to ten with hand seven and missed-UNO penalty on; Chess is one-on-one with Random color; Poker seats two to eight with silent house dealer, stack 1000, small blind 10, and zero-valued no-doubling/no-hand-limit defaults; Pool is one-on-one Race to 1/3/5; Tic-Tac-Toe selects X/O/Random; RPS is best of 3/5/7. Boards enforce legality and use the chat connection for narration. Early End or Resign deletes state and records no winner. Command toggles stop autonomous invitations but not slash or phrase starts.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai treats Conversation Mode as a Marinara product surface. Schedules, call media, emoji libraries, and table-game state are not hosted or converted unless a future adapter proves that support with canonical mappings and fixtures. Kit must not infer codec support from this product guide alone.

### Marinara data and SillyTavern import

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/data-and-interop`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/data-and-interop.md`
- Documents Marinara data layout backup restore and SillyTavern import at the pinned Engine docs. DATA_DIR layout lists storage plus asset folders and the encryption key file or ENCRYPTION_KEY env for API secrets. Backup versus profile contrasts Download Backup full zip restorable via Import Profile Export Profile Native restorable and Export Profile Compatible non-restorable. Restore scans then confirms irreversible import with identity match re-import updates media warnings and secrets stripped requiring re-entered keys and webhooks. SillyTavern single-file import covers Character Chat Preset Lorebook carriers and embedded book prompts. Folder wizard access requires loopback free path remote admin secret or IMPORT_ALLOWED_ROOTS. Folder wizard stages path scan seven categories built-in preset default unchecked tag and regex modes progress and results. Wizard handling rules are best-effort branch grouping group Roleplay import and source date preservation. What does not transfer enumerates global settings Quick Replies non-selected categories and failed items. Bunzai hub conversion is not this wizard.

- **H2 DATA_DIR layout** (`data_dir-layout`)
  DATA_DIR layout holds all user content default data beside the server or Docker app data with a storage log line showing the resolved path after restart. Storage holds characters chats messages lorebooks presets connections as structured records. Asset folders include avatars sprites backgrounds gallery fonts knowledge-sources game-assets custom-emojis custom-stickers for media that is not row storage. The encryption-key file decrypts stored API keys and must travel with data or keys are re-entered; ENCRYPTION_KEY env can replace the file when operators prefer env injection. Mobile backup download includes the key when present so restores stay decryptable on device handoff.
- **H2 Backup versus profile** (`backup-versus-profile`)
  Backup versus profile contrasts three Advanced Backup and Export actions under remote Admin Access when needed. Download Backup is a full zip of storage settings media RESTORE text and encryption key when present and is restorable via Import Profile accepting backup zip. Export Profile Native yields account plus media as marinara-profile json or zip if large and is restorable through the same Import Profile path. Export Profile Compatible is a zip of plain character persona lore for other tools and is not Import Profile restorable so operators must not expect full account recovery from Compatible alone.
- **H2 Restore** (`restore`)
  Restore uses Imports Import Profile for JSON or ZIP packages. Scan then confirm because the step cannot undo once applied. Recent profiles match by item identity so re-import updates in place; very old profiles can duplicate objects instead of merging. Missing media yields amber warning while the rest of the profile still imports. Secrets are stripped from profile exports so authors must re-enter API keys and webhooks after restore before connections work again.
- **H2 SillyTavern single-file import** (`sillytavern-single-file-import`)
  SillyTavern single-file import under Settings Imports accepts Character JSON or PNG Chat JSONL always as Roleplay with switch Preset JSON and Lorebook JSON carriers. Embedded character books may prompt standalone lorebook import after card intake. Quick buttons set all tags and regex Character only for batch convenience. Characters panel Import exposes tag mode and regex scope for finer control. Chat list import uses the open mode tab for CONVO RP or GM rather than a mode field inside the JSONL file.
- **H2 Folder wizard access** (`folder-wizard-access`)
  Folder wizard access is free on loopback. A remote browser needs the server admin secret plus Settings Advanced Admin Access. IMPORT_ALLOWED_ROOTS rejects typed paths outside configured roots while Browse and in-app picker paths remain allowed. Disallowed typed roots fail closed.
- **H2 Folder wizard stages** (`folder-wizard-stages`)
  Folder wizard stages begin with path to ST root data or public inside then Scan Folder. Authors choose categories Characters Chats Group Chats Presets Lorebooks Backgrounds Personas with built-in ST presets default deterministic neutral universal star starting unchecked so bulk import does not silently take stock templates. Tag mode is All No or Existing only default All and Regex is Character only or Global. Progress runs per item then results show counts per-item failure lines and Done.
- **H2 Wizard handling rules** (`wizard-handling-rules`)
  Wizard handling is best-effort, so one failure records a warning and continues. Several chat files for one character import as branches of one chat. Group chats always import as Roleplay. Imported items retain the source last-changed date rather than the import time.
- **H2 What does not transfer** (`what-does-not-transfer`)
  What does not transfer enumerates the documented non-transfer list without deferring to vague upstream prose. Only the seven scanned categories are read by the wizard. Global app settings and Quick Replies are not read and not imported under any category toggle. Built-in ST presets stay out unless the author explicitly checks them. Failed conversion items are skipped and listed in warnings rather than aborting the whole batch. Secrets and non-selected categories do not come over into Marinara storage.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai hub-and-spoke conversion is not Marinara's folder-import wizard. Kit must not claim that Bunzai runs this ST bulk importer, restores Marinara profiles, or preserves DATA_DIR encryption keys without a dedicated implemented path and tests. The wizard's categories, access rules, and loss boundaries describe upstream Marinara behavior only.

### Marinara lorebooks

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/lorebooks`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/lorebooks.md`
- Documents complete Marinara lorebook lifecycle at the pinned Engine docs. What a lorebook is defines keyword injection without API plus opt-in semantic matching. Library panel covers create import multi-export folders OFF badge and categories World Character NPC Spellbook Other. Activation how a book becomes active separates Global mutually exclusive with links Linked character or persona activation and Chat-pinned books plus Enabled kill switch. Overview defaults list Scan Depth Token Budget Entry Limit Max Depth Recursive and Vectors with defaults. Entries cover keys types Selective logic position depth order probability timing flags folders and recursion. Budgets and skips name per-book and chat-wide caps Entry Limit trim order and Active Context skip reasons. Semantic search covers embedding sources query score vector limits and quiet failures. Linking and ownership separates shared linked embedded promoted and chat-pinned books. Import export names native marinara json compatible folderless JSON bulk native zip date preservation and required re-vectorization after import. Bunzai mapping is adapter-specific.

- **H2 What a lorebook is** (`what-a-lorebook-is`)
  A lorebook or World Info holds entries with keys and content. Matching recent chat injects eligible content into the prompt. Keyword matching needs no API. Semantic matching is opt-in per book and requires embeddings when enabled. This runtime behavior does not by itself prove cross-format conversion.
- **H2 Library panel** (`library-panel`)
  Library panel provides New Import Select multi-export delete search by name description linked names tags sort A-Z Z-A Newest Oldest Token Budget Copy Delete library folders separate from in-book entry folders OFF badge when disabled and picture upload. Categories child documents World Character NPC Spellbook Other labels and All Active per-category tabs. Category does not change activation rules; Active means relevant to the open chat.
  - **H3 Categories** (`categories`)
    Categories assign each book one label World Character NPC Spellbook or Other. Overview may label Other as Uncategorized. Tabs are All Active relevant to open chat and per-category filters. Category is organizational and never changes activation rules by itself.
- **H2 Activation (how a book becomes active)** (`activation-how-a-book-becomes-active`)
  Activation how a book becomes active uses three paths. Global Overview switch applies every chat while enabled and is mutually exclusive with character or persona links so enabling Global clears links on save. Linked books auto-activate in chats that include that entity. Pinned books attach to one chat via chat Settings Lorebooks. Enabled must be on or no entries fire even if global or linked. Chat Settings can edit the active list.
- **H2 Overview defaults** (`overview-defaults`)
  Overview defaults are Scan Depth two for recent messages zero whole chat Token Budget two thousand forty-eight zero unlimited Entry Limit one hundred range one to one thousand Max Depth three recursive passes range one to ten Enabled on Recursive off Vectors off for semantic embeddings. Treat these as book-level starting values not chat-wide caps.
- **H2 Entries** (`entries`)
  Entries autosave with states and require a name. Content and keys cover Primary Keys Secondary Keys Description matching toggles and token estimates. Types are Normal Constant Selective with secondary AND Any All NOT Any NOT All. Position depth order probability set injection placement and chance. Timing covers Sticky Cooldown Delay Ephemeral. Additional entry flags from entries guide include Role at Depth group tag filters Locked No Vector context filters and per-entry Recursion. Folders group entries with folder enable clone delete multi-select.
  - **H3 Content and keys** (`content-and-keys`)
    Content and keys use Primary Keys as chip keywords default case-insensitive substring. Content is injected text with macros and live token estimate. Secondary Keys are Selective only. Description is Knowledge Router only never main AI content. Matching toggles Whole Words Case Sensitive Regex with safety timeout and slow pattern failure for that scan.
  - **H3 Types** (`types`)
    Types are Normal primary keys match Constant no keyword still respecting timing probability filters budgets and Selective primary plus secondary logic AND Any AND All NOT Any NOT All. Type choice changes activation cost and should match author intent.
  - **H3 Position, depth, order, probability** (`position-depth-order-probability`)
    Position depth order probability set Position Before chat After chat or at Depth default depth four Order default one hundred lower earlier and Probability default one hundred percent. Depth placement interacts with Role flags when using at Depth insertion.
  - **H3 Timing** (`timing`)
    Timing fields are Sticky Cooldown Delay in messages and Ephemeral in activations with zero meaning off. They gate how often Constant or matched entries reappear across the chat window.
  - **H3 Additional entry flags (from entries guide)** (`additional-entry-flags-from-entries-guide`)
    Additional entry flags from entries guide include Role for at Depth insertion group or tag filters Locked No Vector skip semantic context filters and per-entry Recursion whether this entry text can chain. These are distinct from book-level Recursive switch.
- **H2 Budgets and skips** (`budgets-and-skips`)
  Budgets and skips use per-book Token Budget and chat-wide Lorebook Token Budget chat Settings default eight thousand one hundred ninety-two zero unlimited. Entry Limit is a count cap separate from tokens. Trim order Constants first then matches on latest message then remaining injection order. After a skip later smaller entries may still fit. Active Context lists skips with reasons lorebook budget chat budget or both shows keywords size budget used and may suggest Knowledge Retrieval or Router agents. Recursion does not bypass budgets or Entry Limit.
- **H2 Semantic search** (`semantic-search`)
  Semantic search requires an embedding source connection embedding model example text-embedding-3-small or Local Model sidecar when installed Lite may hide local. Vectors switch per book. Settings Query Messages default ten zero full history Score Threshold default zero point three Vector Limit default ten semantic matches only. Vectorize missing or re-vectorize with confirm. Dimension mismatch or missing vectors fail quietly for those entries while keywords still work. Semantic and keyword matches share budget priority with configured order breaking ties.
- **H2 Linking and ownership** (`linking-and-ownership`)
  Linking and ownership separates shared linked library books attached to characters or personas from embedded books that travel inside a character card when export includes them. Character import can promote an embedded book to a standalone linked Marinara lorebook or leave it only inside the card. Chat-pinned books are chat-owned activation not library membership. Global versus linked mutual exclusion remains. Manage embedded books from character Lorebook tab and Native export which keeps attached lorebooks.
- **H2 Import / export** (`import-export`)
  Import export detects Marinara native versus foreign World Info or V2 character-book JSON automatically. Import single or bulk drop shows per-file success or error rows and preserves created date from the source file. Export Marinara Native marinara json includes folders and every field or Compatible JSON folderless World Info drops Marinara-only details. Bulk export Select Export marinara-lorebooks zip always Native. ST folder wizard can pull lorebooks with other categories. After import child documents re-vectorization requirements for semantic search.
  - **H3 After import** (`after-import`)
    After import keywords work immediately. Semantic search requires re-vectorization after import using Vectors plus Vectorize missing or Re-vectorize. Old vectors from another install are not assumed valid for the new embedding model.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai canonical lorebook mapping is adapter-specific. Marinara token budgets, recursive controls, semantic vectors, folder structure, timing fields, and activation ownership do not automatically round-trip through every target. Kit must consult the active format adapter and report unsupported fields instead of presenting cross-format export as silently lossless.

### Marinara documented macro catalog

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/macro-reference`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/macro-reference.md`
- Exhaustive catalog of every built-in prompt macro named by Pasta-Devs/Marinara-Engine prompts/macros.md at commit b7545a63e7e264a1cd9eeea1a5490d50c08ddb29. Built-ins are case-insensitive; unknown letter-number-underscore names resolve as preset variables or remain literal; the page does not invent extension macros; live Macro Reference and slash macros are engine authorities. Identity and card macros list user userName userNamePhonetic char charName charNamePhonetic characters group persona personaDescription personaPersonality personaBackstory personaAppearance personaScenario description personality backstory appearance scenario example charSysInfo charPostHistory with defaults and group first-character behavior. Conversation Mode only covers convo_display char_about persona_about convo_behavior emptying outside that mode. Conversation placement move suppress auto-insert covers context status commands commandList reactRules emojiReact replyRules memories memoryRecall lorebook lore and singular about placement rules. Context and request macros list input model chatId lastGenerationType idle_duration gameStoryboardKeyframeCount agent type resolved last. Time macros share one browser-timezone timestamp for date time datetime isotime weekday timezone. Random and dice cover random ranges weighted options and roll XdY. Reply-scoped variables setvar getvar addvar incvar decvar reset each generation with setvar-in-random failure mode. Formatting macros cover newline trim case wrap noop comment banned. Non-macros and failure modes plus Conditionals pointer complete the contract.

- **H2 Identity and card macros** (`identity-and-card-macros`)
  Identity and card macros: user and userName return active user or persona display name defaulting to User; userNamePhonetic falls back to user when empty. char and charName return current character name defaulting to Character; charNamePhonetic falls back to char. characters is all chat characters comma-joined; group is other active group characters excluding current responder and excludes persona, following current speaker during group generations. persona joins Description Personality Backstory Appearance Scenario by newlines; personaDescription personaPersonality personaBackstory personaAppearance personaScenario return single persona fields. description personality backstory appearance scenario example return character fields with solo current character or group first character by default unless group blocks iterate. charSysInfo is Character System Prompt; charPostHistory is Character Post-History Instructions.
- **H2 Conversation Mode only** (`conversation-mode-only`)
  Conversation Mode only macros are convo_display char_about persona_about and convo_behavior. They resolve only in Conversation Mode authoring surfaces and remain empty or unused in Roleplay and Game. Pair them with Convo tab fields on characters and personas rather than inventing Roleplay injection paths.
- **H2 Conversation placement (move + suppress auto-insert)** (`conversation-placement-move-suppress-auto-insert`)
  Conversation placement (move + suppress auto-insert) macros render a block at the macro site and skip automatic insertion to avoid duplication, Conversation Mode only. context and status place conversation context or status block. commands and commandList place available-commands reminder. reactRules and emojiReact place custom-emoji reaction rules. replyRules places custom-emoji and sticker reply rules. memories and memoryRecall place memory-recall block. lorebook and lore place lorebook injections. In one-character Conversation, placing char_about or persona_about yourself similarly skips the automatic participant About block. Group Conversation keeps the automatic participant block because a singular about macro covers only one participant.
- **H2 Context and request macros** (`context-and-request-macros`)
  Context and request macros: input is the most recent user message available to the prompt. model is current model name when selected. chatId is current chat ID. lastGenerationType labels generation reason with documented examples normal continue regenerate impersonate guided autonomous turn_game preview game_setup lorebook_scan retry_agents as an open set. idle_duration is human-readable idle time such as 8 minutes. gameStoryboardKeyframeCount is Game Mode keyframes-per-turn target 1-6 default 3 as narrative target not a hard paragraph quota. agent double-colon TYPE returns saved output of agent type TYPE and is resolved last so agent text cannot inject further macros.
- **H2 Time macros** (`time-macros`)
  Time macros all share one resolution timestamp with timezone from the browser. date is YYYY-MM-DD. time is HH:MM 24-hour. datetime and isotime are full timestamps with timezone offset and the same meaning. weekday is weekday name. timezone is timezone name. Do not invent per-macro clock skew; treat them as one snapshot.
- **H2 Random and dice** (`random-and-dice`)
  Random and dice: random alone is integer 0-100 inclusive. random colon X colon Y is integer between X and Y inclusive. random double-colon A double-colon B double-colon C picks one option and macros resolve only inside the chosen option. Weighted form uses option at weight with missing weight equal 1, decimal weights allowed, weight 0 never selected, all weights 0 yields empty, and only a final at-number is a weight. roll colon XdY returns dice total such as 2d6.
- **H2 Reply-scoped variables** (`reply-scoped-variables`)
  Reply-scoped variables exist only for one reply generation and reset every turn. setvar name value stores and emits empty. getvar name reads or empty if unset. addvar name value appends to stored value. incvar name adds 1 to numeric variable. decvar name subtracts 1 from numeric variable. Do not put setvar inside random options because setvar runs for every option before the pick.
- **H2 Formatting macros** (`formatting-macros`)
  Formatting macros: newline and escaped-n insert a line break. trim removes itself and trims surrounding whitespace. trimStart and trimEnd trim start or end of surrounding text. uppercase and lowercase wrap text for case conversion. noop is removed as a harmless placeholder. double-slash comment is an author note removed from output. banned quoted text is removed and does not filter or ban model output.
- **H2 Lifecycle rules (pinned)** (`lifecycle-rules-pinned`)
  Lifecycle rules pinned state that built-in macros need no API key and resolve inside Marinara while preset variables need a defining preset. Agent type macros stay empty until that agent has produced saved output and resolve last so agent text cannot inject further macros. Variables evaluate left to right in one prompt build so early lore setvar can be read later. Random resolves macros only inside the chosen option except setvar addvar incvar decvar which run for every option before the pick. Reply-scoped variables reset every generation. Weighted random missing weight equals one weight zero never selected all-zero weights yield empty.
- **H2 Non-macros and failure modes** (`non-macros-and-failure-modes`)
