  Non-macros and failure modes: prompt alone opens Peek Prompt and is not a resolving macro. No escape character for braces; unknown names stay literal unless a preset variable matches. Custom Tools do not resolve macros. Impersonate templates accept only a restricted placeholder set, not the full list. Very large or deeply nested expansions are cut off silently. Live slash macros and Macro Reference may update with the engine; this catalog is the pinned static floor and must not invent extension macros.
- **H2 Conditionals** (`conditionals`)
  Conditionals: operators live on Prompts and macros including if blocks, else, equality and comparison operators, and logical and or. They are part of the macro system and apply wherever macros work. This catalog does not re-list operator tables; treat that page as the normative operator reference and this page as the exhaustive name catalog.

### Marinara maps, game assets, HUDs, and storyboards

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/maps-and-game-content`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/maps-and-game-content.md`
- Documents Marinara hierarchical maps, Game assets, sessions, HUD widgets, party and NPC state, storyboards, Conversation-branched scenes, Roleplay trackers, and Narrative Director at the pinned Engine docs. Maps cover package and per-chat activation, authoring fields, AI draft review, explicit enable and save, exact-location lore, one-move travel, bindings, import, archive, and history-safe IDs. Game assets cover five categories, browser lifecycle, exact formats and limits, music path grammar, protected starter content, per-game folder selection, and local/admin device folders. Sessions cover immutable setup sharing, conclude and resume, read-only replay, editable history, spoiler state, autosave, and non-restorable checkpoints. Game and Roleplay HUDs remain distinct. Party, Scene, storyboard, and Secret Plot sections preserve their separate ownership and destructive boundaries. Bunzai makes no unproven game-state conversion claim.

- **H2 Hierarchical maps (Roleplay and Game)** (`hierarchical-maps-roleplay-and-game`)
  Hierarchical maps Roleplay and Game require package install via Download Agents restart and per-chat enable not global. Limits are up to five hundred locations and twenty hierarchy levels preferring wide sibling floors. Each location has parent children siblings type Region Settlement Place Building Floor Room public description private AI notes lorebook entries on exact location and one-way or two-way links any-to-any with children as list positioned map or ordered layers. Workflow Create map Draft with AI Draft preview search select regenerate Continue to editor set start location Enabled plus Save. Draft and import only change working copy until enable and save. Travel Story map set destination next message commits move; AI cannot relocate by narration alone. Path details nearby destinations and eligible lore can enter context. Import export of map files is chat-owned map state.
- **H2 Game assets** (`game-assets`)
  Game assets have Music Ambient Sound Effects Sprites and Backgrounds categories. Asset Browser offers folder tree and breadcrumbs, grid or list, search, upload and drop, subfolders, rename, move, copy, delete, download, multi-select, notes, pagination, and rescan. Bundled CC0 starter assets are protected from move and delete; backgrounds start empty. Audio accepts MP3 OGG WAV FLAC M4A AAC WebM; sprites accept PNG JPG JPEG GIF WebP AVIF SVG; backgrounds exclude SVG. Audio and images cap at 50 MB, text at 10 MB. Music requires state genre intensity with four states, ten genres, and three intensities. Each Game may exclude folders without deleting them. Music DJ can use a Game Assets path or local device folder, with local or admin gating for device access. This is separate from Roleplay and Conversation media.
- **H2 Sessions and saves** (`sessions-and-saves`)
  Game campaigns use numbered sessions. End Session accepts an optional next-session direction up to 5000 characters and generates a conclusion; New Session resumes from its summary and direction. Initial Game Setup is an immutable shareable snapshot of adventure, cast, model, prompt, effective parameters, visuals, storyboards, and world tools, excluding credentials endpoints keys database IDs and bundled content. Completed entries can include summaries, resume, party dynamics, discoveries, character moments, recall details, NPC updates, requested continuation, stats, and party status. Replay is read-only and recorded-choice only. Edit Details changes notes; Regenerate replaces them; Update Plot Arcs revises hidden plans. Show Spoilers reveals and edits hidden world and campaign state with invalid JSON rejected. State autosaves and checkpoints at session and combat boundaries, but checkpoints have no restore UI. Custom HUDs are reviewed before session start.
- **H2 HUD widgets (Game)** (`hud-widgets-game`)
  HUD widgets Game default on in setup wizard with up to four left and right. Types are Progress Bar Gauge Relationship Meter Counter Stat Block List Inventory Grid Timer. Pre-session Review Starting Widgets edit or remove before Start Game and Prepare Next Session Widgets on continue. During play GM updates manual edit collapse lock unlock drag double-click lock or R resets position. Layout is per game with import export of widget layouts where documented. Roleplay uses separate HUD trackers not the Game widget set.
- **H2 Party and NPCs** (`party-and-npcs`)
  Party bar sheets hold identity, level, class, HP, status, attributes, abilities, and inventory, with AI regenerate and manual edit. Recruit and remove change Game membership rather than Roleplay group-chat membership. Adventure Journal separates generated story records from Player Notes. NPC reputation has seven ordered labels from Hostile to Ally, with detail and meter views. Game Master modes govern NPC visibility and private notes without converting NPCs into user-controlled party members.
- **H2 Storyboards (Game only)** (`storyboards-game-only`)
  Storyboards Game only run after GM narration turn producing one to six keyframes default three from stripped GM tags. Stills go to Gallery Images and clips to Videos. Requires Game Illustrator image connection with error if missing. Manual Create storyboard and Automatic Storyboard Illustrations after each turn wizard default on when Visual Generation on. Expose prompts means manual reviews each keyframe while automatic does not pause. Send Avatar References improves consistency. gameStoryboardKeyframeCount supplies target count to GM prompts.
- **H2 Scenes** (`scenes`)
  A Scene is a temporary Roleplay branched from Conversation with slash scene or slash rp, POV, tense, instructions, its own background, stage, opening, and messages. The origin retains an active-scene link. End Scene writes a generated memory summary to the origin; Discard deletes without write-back; Convert detaches it into a permanent Roleplay without summary or memory. Clone from here copies through one message while keeping the original active. Scenes deliberately exclude Connected Chat steering and history so they remain self-contained; use Connected Chats for continuous influence. Gallery media stays owned by the Scene chat and is distinct from its backdrop.
- **H2 Trackers and Secret Plot** (`trackers-and-secret-plot`)
  Roleplay has World State, Persona Stats, Present Characters, Inventory, Active Quests, and Custom Tracker widgets. Popovers save manual edits immediately. Lock mode protects chosen values; schedules and refresh rerun one or all trackers; Clear Trackers is irreversible. The larger Tracker Panel adds custom field structure, add delete lock and hide modes. Hiding Mood Look Outfit or Thoughts clears and locks it; omitted agent output does not erase author fields. Narrative Director is Roleplay-only. Push Story arms one Natural or Random Event instruction for the next reply. Secret Plot is off by default and updates a hidden arc every 1 to 100 assistant replies, default 8. Reveal supports arc, protagonist, one character, completion, save, and confirmed regenerate. Clearing runs or memory retains the arc; removing Narrative Director deletes it irreversibly.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai makes no claim of full Marinara game-state conversion. Hierarchical maps, asset selection, sessions, widgets, storyboards, scenes, party state, trackers, and Secret Plot remain platform-owned unless a future adapter maps each field and lifecycle through the canonical model with round-trip fixtures. Unknown state must be escrowed or reported rather than silently flattened.

### Marinara prompts and macros

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/prompts-and-macros`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/prompts-and-macros.md`
- Documents Marinara macro rules, conditionals, preset variables, the full prompt-preset lifecycle, media prompt overrides, Chat Settings Presets, and generation controls at the pinned Engine docs. It routes exact built-in names to the macro catalog while preserving evaluation rules and live /macros authority. Prompt presets cover panel organization, explicit save and dirty-exit behavior, Overview Sections Prompts tabs, live markers, groups, wrap formats, depth placement, assignment, Conversation and Game single-prompt differences, and Peek Prompt verification. Media overrides cover registered image and video templates, variables, preview, active paused default states, and reset. Chat Settings Presets bundle connection, prompt source, agents, tools, translation, memory, and advanced parameters while excluding chat content, with mode defaults and JSON interchange. Generation controls give ranges, Send defaults, provider extras, and baseline to connection to chat precedence. Bunzai preserves macro text without executing it.

- **H2 Macro system rules** (`macro-system-rules`)
  Macro system rules use syntax double-brace tags; built-ins are case-insensitive and always on in macro-enabled fields. Fields include character and persona prose and advanced prompts, lorebook Description and Content, preset sections, regex Find Replace Trim, agent templates, and chat input. Nested macros resolve. Custom Tools do not resolve macros. Reply-scoped variables reset every generation; setvar inside random options runs for all options. Unknown NAME becomes a preset variable if defined else left literal under the literal braces strategy. The prompt tag alone opens Peek Prompt rather than expansion. Impersonate templates use a restricted placeholder set. Huge nested expansions may be silently truncated. Live Macro Reference UI and slash macros and macro commands are engine-generated authorities that outrank static documentation for installation-specific discovery.
- **H2 Conditionals** (`conditionals`)
  Conditionals use if cond, else if, else, and end-if blocks. Operators are equality double-equals equals is, inequality not-equals is not, numeric greater less greater-or-equal less-or-equal, and contains or includes with not-forms. Equality uses numbers if both numeric else case-insensitive text. Numeric compares are false if non-numeric. Logical and binds before or; parentheses are allowed. Compact equality allows character equals A or B style lists. Group blocks iterate multi-character contexts per conditional-prompts. Truthy checks without operator are supported. First true branch wins; discarded branches are not resolved.
- **H2 Preset variables** (`preset-variables`)
  Preset variables exist on section-based presets only, not Conversation single-override mode. Names use letters numbers underscores. Kinds are single-choice, boolean toggle with one option, multi-select, and multi-select plus random pick. Options have Label and Value. Configure modal on assign offers Save as default, Skip, or Confirm; Confirm requires every single-choice filled. Edit preset variables from chat settings. Switching presets clears choices. Do not treat preset variables as free global env for every chat mode.
- **H2 Preset editor** (`preset-editor`)
  The Presets panel supports create, JSON import, multi-select export or delete, search, four sort orders, folders, and a starred default. New presets require a name; edits do not autosave and dirty exit offers Keep editing, Discard, or Save and close. Overview holds name, description, author, and XML Markdown or None wrap format. Sections combine macro-aware Prompt Blocks with live Character Info, Persona, Chat History, Chat Summary, Dialogue Examples, and Lorebook markers. Missing lore markers warn; examples append to Character Info only when no explicit examples marker exists. Sections expose role, enable, duplicate, delete, order, group, and ordered or depth placement. Agent Sections insert the matching agent result. Prompts holds Conversation and Game single prompts while Roleplay uses assembled sections. Roleplay presets require chat assignment. Conversation and Game choose a source, may become chat-local Custom, and Game adds 2000 characters of instructions. Peek Prompt reveals the final request.
- **H2 Prompt overrides** (`prompt-overrides`)
  Prompt Overrides are reusable image and video generation templates under Settings Generations, not chat-prompt section patches. The image registry covers selfies, portraits, sprites, scenes, storyboards, Game NPCs, Noodle images, and other image builders; video covers scenes, Conversation Call clips, and animated expressions. Each editor selects a registered template, shows Default, Custom active, or Custom paused, inserts only listed dollar-brace variables, renders example values, and warns about unknown variables. Save activates the override, Apply this override off preserves but pauses it, and Reset to Default removes it after confirmation. It has no effect unless the corresponding media feature and generation connection run.
- **H2 Chat-settings presets** (`chat-settings-presets`)
  Chat Settings Presets work in Conversation and Roleplay, not Game. They bundle connection, prompt preset or Conversation source, agents, tools, translation, Memory Recall, Advanced Parameters, and related behavior, while excluding characters, persona, lorebooks, sprites, summary, tags, and scene prompt. Applying updates the chat immediately and the picker reports a matching preset, Custom settings, or Missing preset. Actions save into, rename, Save As, JSON import and export, and delete. Import adds a non-default copy without overwrite. One preset per mode can be starred for new chats. Each mode also has an immutable empty Default preset that resets preset-controlled settings to system defaults.
- **H2 Generation parameters** (`generation-parameters`)
  Generation parameters live under Chat Settings Advanced Parameters all modes. Temperature 0-2 start 1 sent No. Max Output Tokens unbounded box start 4096 Conversation 8192 Roleplay Game sent Yes. Top P 0-1 start 1 sent No. Top K 0-500 zero off start 0 sent No. Frequency and Presence -2 to 2 start 0 sent No. Reasoning Effort None Low Medium High Xhigh Maximum start Maximum sent Yes. Verbosity None Low Medium High start High sent No. Send switch off omits the field; only Max Output Tokens and Reasoning Effort start sent. Assistant Prefill optional. Thinking Tags one wrapper per line. Custom Parameters JSON with connection-level then per-chat override. OpenRouter Service Tier Default Flex Priority. Limit Context Messages off default last N start 50 range 1-9999. Exclude Past Reasoning on default. Image Captioning off default. Save as Connection Default writes to connection. Precedence baseline then connection then chat custom keys chat wins.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai maps canonical presets only when adapters define them. Macros are not executed at import or conversion. Preserve sealed macro text as data, report unsupported preset sections, and never invent execution or silent expansion during interchange merely because another platform has template syntax.

### Marinara authoring reference

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/README`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/README.md`
- Entry map for Kit's Marinara Engine authoring corpus. The collection routes characters, personas, and Convo profiles; Conversation schedules and custom media; sprites, galleries, colors, and stats; visual assets and card CSS; lorebooks; prompts, macros, and the exhaustive macro catalog; agents, custom tools, regex, memory, and Professor Mari; chat interchange and group behavior; maps, game state, HUDs, scenes, and storyboards; data layout, backup, and SillyTavern import; and the source-coverage manifest. Authority pins Pasta-Devs/Marinara-Engine commit b7545a63e7e264a1cd9eeea1a5490d50c08ddb29 retrieved 2026-07-26 under AGPL-3.0. Questions should open the narrow owning page and read the complete surrounding section. Upstream behavior never proves Bunzai adapter support: the Marinara format page governs implemented preservation, and SillyTavern macro behavior must not be projected onto Marinara.

- **H2 Collection map** (`collection-map`)
  Collection map lists Characters and personas including Convo profiles Conversation Mode for schedules emojis stickers GIFs calls selfies and table games Sprites and galleries colors and stats Visual assets and card CSS Lorebooks Prompts and macros with Macro catalog Agents tools and regex Chats and groups Maps and game content Data and SillyTavern import and Source coverage. Open the narrow owning page for each artifact type rather than treating the README as the full field contract. Preserve these rules in Kit answers without inventing adapter support.
- **H2 Authority** (`authority`)
  Authority pins Pasta-Devs/Marinara-Engine at commit b7545a63e7e264a1cd9eeea1a5490d50c08ddb29, retrieved 2026-07-26, licensed AGPL-3.0. The Bunzai pages are independently worded rather than a verbatim mirror. Implementation claims about what Bunzai can preserve belong to the Marinara format adapter reference. Do not copy SillyTavern macro semantics onto Marinara macros, conditionals, or preset variables; the platforms differ and false equivalence is a conversion bug.

### Marinara authoring source coverage

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/source-coverage`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/source-coverage.md`
- Path-by-path inventory for all 118 Markdown files at Pasta-Devs/Marinara-Engine commit b7545a63e7e264a1cd9eeea1a5490d50c08ddb29, retrieved 2026-07-26 under AGPL-3.0. Fifty-seven included sources route characters and personas, all seven Conversation guides, sprites and galleries, card CSS and visual assets, every lorebook and prompt guide, agent and memory material, custom tools and regex, Professor Mari, chat interchange and groups, maps and selected Game or Roleplay authored state, data storage, backup, and SillyTavern import to explicit destinations. Sixty-one individually named exclusions cover installation, providers, ordinary app chrome and chat runtime, development internals, integrations, destructive maintenance, onboarding, and media-provider setup with a reason per file. No wildcard substitutes remain, and every Conversation file is included.

- **H2 Included** (`included`)
  Included classifies 57 files explicitly. Character creation, personas, selection, interchange, colors, sprites, and galleries route to character or media references. Every Conversation guide is named and routed. Card CSS, style profiles, scene backgrounds, animated expressions, and Roleplay backgrounds route to visual assets. All six lorebook files, seven prompt files, and seven agent files receive individual destinations, with hierarchical maps routed to maps rather than agents. Custom tools, regex, Professor Mari, four chat guides, five Game guides, three Roleplay state guides, and three data or import guides complete the selected authoring corpus. These upstream destinations do not imply Bunzai codec support.
- **H2 Excluded (path-level)** (`excluded-path-level`)
  Excluded classifies 61 files one row at a time with no directory wildcards. The list names installation and upgrade guides, remote and operator configuration, every provider-connection guide, the settings shell, development and Noodle internals, runtime integrations, destructive data clearing, catalog browsing and library navigation, application appearance, routine chat and live-command UX, runtime Game and Roleplay play guides, onboarding, and media-provider integrations. Each path carries a reason, while all seven Conversation files remain included rather than hidden in a folder bucket.

### Marinara sprites, galleries, colors, and stats

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/sprites-and-galleries`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/sprites-and-galleries.md`
- Documents Marinara sprites, galleries, exact color fields, and roleplay statistics at the pinned Engine revision. Sprites distinguish Facial Expressions, Full-body poses, and call Clips, then cover single and folder upload naming, AI generation prerequisites, transparent-background cleanup, replacement, download, deletion, and arrangement. Galleries are ordered character or persona media with image or optional video lifecycle plus scoped custom-emoji and sticker tagging and validation limits. Color fields are Name Display Color with gradient support, Dialogue Highlight Color, and semi-transparent Message Box Color, including where each renders. Character stats define HP or MP pools and six RPG attributes; persona stats add four status bars and optional RPG attributes, with defaults, visibility, prompt injection, and Character Tracker versus Persona Stats agent ownership. Compatible exports omit these Marinara-only surfaces unless a native package or proven adapter carries them.

- **H2 Sprites** (`sprites`)
  Sprites (Roleplay and Game display) defines two kinds: Facial Expressions for moods and Full-body for poses. Clips are call media and are not this page. Sprites display in Roleplay and Game while assets can still attach in any mode. Upload covers single name upload preferring transparent PNG, quick-add suggested expression names, Upload Folder basenames with happy_01 and happy_blush as variants of happy, and Manage Frame Download Replace Delete plus multi-delete All Expressions or All Full-Body. AI generation needs an image connection and video for animated portraits, with Generate Sprite dialog fields and Android disable reasons. Cleanup and export offers erase paint undo reset previews and file download without assuming PNG card export embeds the full set. Placement shows floating sprites in Roleplay and Game with arrange mode; Conversation does not show sprite art.
- **H2 Galleries** (`galleries`)
  Galleries are ordered media for character or persona with upload of common image formats optional video download and delete per item. Custom emoji and sticker tagging from gallery uses scope profile versus chat and naming size rules matching Conversation custom media limits one to thirty-two char lowercase names max two hundred fifty-six pixels. Failure rejects oversized or invalid format at upload. Galleries are not expression grids and not scene backgrounds. Preserve these rules in Kit answers without inventing adapter support.
- **H2 Colors (exact fields)** (`colors-exact-fields`)
  Colors exact fields leave empty for theme defaults. Extract Colors from Avatar disabled until avatar exists. Name Display Color colors the name and supports CSS gradients such as linear-gradient ninety deg amber to red and also tints sidebar tabs or persona pickers. Dialogue Highlight Color colors quoted dialogue text example gold and works with quote styles optional bold from Settings. Message Box Color is bubble background prefer semi-transparent rgba. Message box applies Conversation and Roleplay. Preserve these rules in Kit answers without inventing adapter support.
- **H2 Stats** (`stats`)
  Stats values are starting defaults for new chats while agents change them during play. Character Enable RPG Stats adds Pools HP MP one hundred of one hundred default add remove color and Attributes STR DEX CON INT WIS CHA default ten. Off shows nothing and sends nothing. Persona Enable Persona Stats status bars Satiety Energy Hygiene Mood one hundred of one hundred and Enable RPG Attributes same pools attributes as characters. Agents Character Tracker updates character RPG and persona RPG attributes Persona Stats agent updates status bars. Without agents defaults stay fixed. HUD shows enabled stats as gradient bars.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Bunzai: cross-format cards usually lose sprites, galleries, colors, and stats unless a native Marinara pack is used. Report those losses honestly during conversion. Do not invent PNG or CCv2 fidelity for Marinara-only media grids, emoji scopes, color fields, or game stats that only travel in native packages.

### Marinara visual assets and card CSS

- id: `reference/platforms/marinara/visual-and-css`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/marinara/visual-and-css.md`
- Documents Marinara card CSS, image style profiles, scene and Roleplay background lifecycles, and animated expression generation at the pinned Engine docs. Card CSS covers Creator Notes ownership, active-character control visibility, Disabled Exclusive Chat scopes, sanitizer boundaries, layout-specific selectors, and Native versus Tavern export. Style Profiles covers ten built-ins, clone reset delete, four prompt grammars, per-kind tags, validation, cleaning, and app to connection to chat precedence. Backgrounds distinguish Gallery generation from library selection and the non-generating Background agent, including modes, connection fallback, prompt review, upload formats and ownership, per-image actions, chat pinning, cross-fade, and solid-color fallback. Animated expressions cover video plus ffmpeg requirements, per-expression partial success, fixed 9:16 GIF output, duration, missing cleanup and transparency guarantee, and explicit failure modes. Bunzai does not claim visual round-trip fidelity it has not proven.

- **H2 Card CSS theming** (`card-css-theming`)
  Card CSS theming embeds style blocks in character or persona Creator Notes. Chat Settings Card Theming appears only when an active character in the chat has CSS persona CSS alone does not show the control. Modes Disabled default Exclusive each character CSS only its messages Chat all card CSS affects whole chat area. Engine sanitizes dangerous CSS scopes to chat and injects over app message styles. Selectors must stay under data-card-css chat surfaces message bubble name content. Linear Conversation layout has no mari-message-bubble while Bubbles and Roleplay do. Forbidden styling outside chat breakout to app chrome unsafe constructs stripped. Export ownership CSS rides Creator Notes on Marinara Native character persona export compatible CCv2 PNG does not preserve custom CSS as a first-class field.
- **H2 Style profiles** (`style-profiles`)
  Style profiles live under Settings Generations Image Generation Style Profiles. Ten built-ins Off Auto default Anime Danbooru Illustrious Realistic SDXL Photorealistic Cinematic Digital Painting Painterly Fantasy Z-Image Turbo Narrative. Clone creates editable copy and sets it default Reset restores built-ins Delete user clones only when more than one profile exists. Prompt grammar Hybrid Danbooru tags Tags Natural language. Style text positive negative tags per-image-kind tags avatar portrait selfie background illustration sprite. Test bench validates. Precedence app default connection override chat override later wins. Cleaning regenerates tags from style text.
- **H2 Scene backgrounds and Roleplay backgrounds** (`scene-backgrounds-and-roleplay-backgrounds`)
  Scene background generation works in Roleplay and Game, not Conversation. Gallery Background builds a 1280 by 720 default prompt from current setting, location, weather, and time, uses the Illustrator connection or image default, saves to the background library, and applies immediately; missing connection fails. Gallery conditionally exposes Illustrate, Selfie, Background, Video, storyboard, and image browsing. Per-image actions open, pin, download, animate, copy saved prompt, and confirm delete. Expose media prompts opens editable positive and negative prompt review with kind, size, count, required non-empty text, Cancel, and Generate. The library accepts JPG PNG GIF WebP and AVIF up to 20 MB; user assets can rename tag delete while built-ins are read-only. Roleplay may pin a chat background or let the optional Background agent select existing library images after replies. That agent never generates. Backdrops cross-fade and fall back to solid color.
- **H2 Animated expressions** (`animated-expressions`)
  Animated expressions are looping GIF portrait sprites selected like still expressions by Expression Engine. Generation requires a Video Generation connection and ffmpeg. Character or persona Facial Expressions switches to animated mode, takes appearance and selected expressions, generates one fixed 9:16 clip at a time, converts locally, and preserves successful results when another expression fails. Duration is 1 to 8 seconds default 3 and output is 512 pixels wide. GIF output skips sheet slicing, cropping, and background cleanup; the transparent-style option is only a prompt hint and cannot guarantee transparency. Prompt exposure reviews every expression. Missing ffmpeg or video connection fails explicitly, and platforms unable to load the image library disable the generator.
- **H2 Bunzai** (`bunzai`)
  Native Marinara export may carry CSS in Creator Notes and media folders. Compatible Tavern PNG does not prove preservation of Marinara-specific CSS, background libraries, animated expressions, style profiles, or generation settings. Bunzai reports unsupported visual ownership rather than executing media or claiming a silent full-fidelity round trip.

## reference/platforms/sillytavern

### SillyTavern character authoring

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/characters`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/characters.md`
- Defines a SillyTavern character as both a portable Character Card and a runtime prompt component, with character name as the only required authoring field. Identity and permanent definitions separates always-sent name, description, personality, and scenario from conditional material, explains their context cost, and preserves authored prose rather than normalizing writing styles. Greetings and dialogue examples covers First Message as a style demonstration, Alternate Greetings as opening swipes, and START example blocks that may leave context as budget tightens. Advanced definitions and metadata distinguishes Main Prompt and Post-History Instructions overrides composed with original, depth-based Character's Note, group talkativeness, and non-prompt attribution fields. Lore, attachments, and platform links separates embedded books, named World Info links, chat lore, Data Bank attachments, tags, and installation state. Character expressions (sprites) documents per-emotion files under the character folder, ZIP packs, classifiers, custom labels, multi-sprite naming, folder override, and the rule that PNG cards do not carry the full expression grid. Import, export, and Bunzai boundaries explains JSON and PNG card layers, canonical mapping plus escrow, cross-format loss reporting, and the rule that scripts and installation-specific links remain sealed rather than transplanted or executed.

- **H2 Identity and permanent definitions** (`identity-and-permanent-definitions`)
  Identity and permanent definitions states that name supplies character identity and the char macro, description carries broad durable facts, personality summarizes traits, and scenario establishes circumstances. SillyTavern sends all four on every generation, so their tokens reduce space available for history, examples, World Info, and retrieved documents. Authors should reserve them for stable required facts and move conditional material into lore, Author's Note, or depth-based injections. The prose fields accept free text, structured lists, and pseudo-dialogue without a mandated mini-language; actual effect depends on the model and surrounding template. Bunzai preserves authored strings without converting one writing method into another.
- **H2 Greetings and dialogue examples** (`greetings-and-dialogue-examples`)
  Greetings and dialogue examples distinguishes three teaching layers. First Message starts a new chat as the initial assistant turn and strongly demonstrates voice, markdown or HTML markup, pacing, and desired response length. Alternate Greetings provide additional opening swipes and may be selected when initiating group conversations so one card can host several entry scenarios. Example Messages use START blocks plus user and char labels; SillyTavern replaces the marker with the active example separator or utility prompt. Examples are budget-sensitive and can fall out block by block unless settings force retention, so they should demonstrate style rather than contain indispensable facts.
- **H2 Advanced definitions and metadata** (`advanced-definitions-and-metadata`)
  Advanced definitions and metadata separates behavioral instructions from attribution. Main Prompt and Post-History Instructions may override global values when the corresponding preference is enabled, while the original macro composes the default into an override instead of fully replacing it. Character's Note is a role-tagged static injection whose depth controls recency (depth zero after the most recent message; larger values earlier), and talkativeness changes natural-order group response probability rather than prose style. Creator, character version, creator notes, source references, and embedded tags support attribution or discovery and are normally not inserted into the model prompt, so conversion must not fold them into description prose merely because another platform has fewer metadata slots.
- **H2 Lore, attachments, and platform links** (`lore-attachments-and-platform-links`)
  Lore, attachments, and platform links distinguishes an embedded character book that travels inside a compatible card, a named World Info link that depends on a separate book in the receiving installation, and chat lore owned by one conversation. Character-scoped Data Bank attachments are stored locally and do not export with the card. Tags may be assigned on the card, imported from embedded Tags to Embed, or bulk-edited; Manage Tags holds local colors, order, and bogus folder modes, while tag backup JSON is installation-specific. Import can create local tags, attach matches, import all, or import none. Filtering toggles include, exclude, or ignore tags. Character locks, chat history, favorites, and UI layout remain installation state and must not be rewritten into description prose during conversion.
- **H2 Character expressions (sprites)** (`character-expressions-sprites`)
  Character expressions (sprites) stores per-emotion images under data user-handle characters character_name, separate from the single card avatar. Authors upload per emotion or a flat ZIP sprite pack that does not rename files to match emotions. Manual display uses click, expression-set, or Quick Reply; automatic change uses Local classifier (one-time model download; 28-label default or 6-label via extensions.models.classification), Main API LLM classification (Limited or Full Context), WebLLM, or deprecated Extras classify. Custom Expressions add author-named labels; Local and Extras classifiers know fixed sets unless trained or LLM sees defaults plus customs. Formats include PNG, webp, and animated gifs. Fallback may be an expression, No Fallback, or Default emojis. Multi-sprite naming uses expression name then a suffix after a dot or hyphen. Sprite Folder Override or costume command selects image sets. Portable PNG cards must not claim to carry the full expression grid.
- **H2 Import, export, and Bunzai boundaries** (`import-export-and-bunzai-boundaries`)
  Import, export, and Bunzai boundaries describes portable Character Card JSON and PNG carriers imported from files or URLs. Image pixels are presentation while encoded card data is the semantic record. Bunzai maps supported fields into a canonical character while retaining the complete source snapshot in escrow for same-format restoration of SillyTavern-specific fields that lack a canonical editing surface. Cross-format output carries only target-supported semantics and must report losses. Regex scripts, automation identifiers, local World Info names, and other installation-specific links cannot be copied merely because another platform offers a vaguely similar field. Imported scripts remain sealed data and never execute during import or conversion.

### SillyTavern chats and group configuration

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/chats-and-groups`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/chats-and-groups.md`
- Treats SillyTavern chats and group chats as re-importable creative artifacts documented at pin 70e5e4d3c239253fca4692fe82e3936cb9c4b1b1 via chatfilemanagement and groupchats sources. Solo versus group chats contrasts one-card solo chats with Create New Chat Group multi-member shared history. Chat import and export covers Import sources from Character.AI and other tools, Export as JSONL as re-importable metadata-bearing files excluding images, Export as TXT as human-readable non-reimportable dumps, and Checkpoints and rename including Create Branch, Create Checkpoint, parent links, and rename breakage. Group reply order strategies enumerates Manual, Natural Order with talkativeness and self-response rules, List Order, and Pooled Order. Group generation handling always shares history and selects Swap character cards or Join character cards with mute force auto, Allow Self Responses, Group Chat Scenario Override, Peek Character Definitions, and membership edits. Bunzai boundary states Bunzai does not host SillyTavern chat runtime, documents interchange for Kit understanding, keeps chat JSONL conversion out of scope without a future adapter, and leaves talkativeness on the character card.

- **H2 Solo versus group chats** (`solo-versus-group-chats`)
  Solo versus group chats states a solo chat opens from one character card. A group chat is created with Create New Chat Group and holds multiple member cards that share history while reply drafting and card-assembly rules apply. The distinction is structural: group mode introduces reply order strategies, generation handling, mute force auto controls, and scenario overrides that do not apply to a single-card solo conversation. Authors must treat group membership and shared history as part of the stored chat artifact, not mere temporary UI selection.
- **H2 Chat import and export** (`chat-import-and-export`)
  Chat import and export separates re-importable structured interchange from lossy human dumps and branching clones. Import sources lists Character.AI via CAI Tools plus TavernAI, oobabooga text-generation-webui, Agnai, KoboldAI Lite, and RisuAI with source-dependent fidelity. Export as JSONL produces re-importable files with metadata but excluding images and file attachments, leaving privacy scrubbing to the sharer. Export as TXT is human-readable and cannot be re-imported; metadata is lost. Checkpoints and rename covers Create Branch (clone and switch), Create Checkpoint (clone name without switch), parent links by chat file name, Back to parent chat, and the failure mode that renaming breaks checkpoint links that stored the old file name. Default names use start date and time; rename via the pencil control.
  - **H3 Import sources** (`import-sources`)
    Import sources states SillyTavern can import chats from Character.AI via the CAI Tools extension and from tools including TavernAI, oobabooga text-generation-webui, Agnai, KoboldAI Lite, and RisuAI. Import formats and fidelity depend on the source tool. Do not assume byte-identical or field-complete transfer across every origin.
  - **H3 Export as JSONL** (`export-as-jsonl`)
    Export as JSONL is available from Manage chat files for each chat. The export produces a re-importable .jsonl including metadata but excluding images and file attachments. Privacy scrubbing before share is the user's responsibility. Treat JSONL as the portable chat interchange format when re-import is required.
  - **H3 Export as TXT** (`export-as-txt`)
    Export as TXT is a plain-text download intended for human reading. It cannot be re-imported into SillyTavern as a chat. Metadata is lost. Never present TXT as a lossless interchange path for chat history, checkpoints, or group configuration.
  - **H3 Checkpoints and rename** (`checkpoints-and-rename`)
    Checkpoints and rename defines Create Branch as cloning the chat up to a message and switching to the clone, and Create Checkpoint as cloning up to a message, naming it, without switching. Checkpoints link to the parent by chat file name; Back to parent chat returns from a checkpoint. Renaming a chat breaks checkpoint links that stored the old file name. Default chat file names use start date and time; rename via the pencil control.
- **H2 Group reply order strategies** (`group-reply-order-strategies`)
  Group reply order strategies enumerates four stored modes. Manual: the user picks the speaker via menu or trigger; empty user input can trigger a random unmuted member; user messages do not auto-trigger replies. Natural Order: extract whole-word name mentions from the last message (self-mentions ignored unless Allow Self Responses); then activate by Talkativeness where 0 percent never unless mentioned, 100 percent always, default 50 percent; if none activated, pick random. List Order: draft strictly by member list order. Pooled Order: pick a random member who has not spoken since the last user message; when all have spoken, pick randomly until the next user message. These strategies are group configuration, not character prose.
- **H2 Group generation handling** (`group-generation-handling`)
  Group generation handling always shares history among members. Card assembly modes are Swap character cards (default: only the active speaker's card enters context) and Join character cards (combine all members' cards in list order, hurting isolation and risking identity confusion). Join sub-modes include or exclude muted members. Combined fields are Description; Scenario unless chat override; Personality; Message examples; Character notes and Depth prompts. Join Prefix and Suffix can separate fields; macros and char and FIELDNAME replacement apply. Mute disables replies; Force Talk triggers one member even if muted; Auto-mode chains generations with a five-second delay and disables when the user types though queued gens may continue; Allow Self Responses affects Natural Order self-mentions; Group Chat Scenario Override replaces each card's scenario for the group and branches inherit then can diverge; Peek Character Definitions edits the card itself; members can be added, removed, or reordered.
- **H2 Bunzai boundary** (`bunzai-boundary`)
  Bunzai boundary states Bunzai does not host SillyTavern chat runtime. The page documents interchange and group configuration so Kit understands what stored artifacts mean. Conversion of chat JSONL is out of scope unless a future adapter exists. Character talkativeness remains a card field documented on Characters rather than a free-floating group-only value. Do not claim Bunzai can run group generation, auto-mode chains, or re-import ST chats without an explicit adapter contract.

### SillyTavern macro syntax and operators

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/macro-operators`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/macro-operators.md`
- Complete operational model of the pinned SillyTavern macro parser. It specifies case-insensitive brace tokens, single and multi-argument separators, legacy colon syntax, ignored definition whitespace, inner-first nesting, scoped final arguments, trimming and dedenting, preserved whitespace, truthiness, inversion, else branches, implemented and planned flags, comments, and literal-brace escaping. Variable sections distinguish chat-local and installation-global prefixes, identifier restrictions, nested assigned values, and every shorthand operator with mutation, comparison, fallback, lazy evaluation, and return behavior. Full-form equivalence covers get, set, add, increment, and decrement, while existence and deletion remain named macros. Legacy angle markers map to modern forms without authoring-time normalization. The safety contract makes field registry and processing stage part of semantics and keeps all macro payloads sealed during Bunzai import and conversion.

- **H2 Tokens, arguments, whitespace, and nesting** (`tokens-arguments-whitespace-and-nesting`)
  Tokens, arguments, whitespace, and nesting defines case-insensitive double-brace names, space-separated single arguments, recommended double-colon multiple arguments, and backward-compatible single-colon syntax. Whitespace around names, separators, and arguments does not affect parsing. Nested expressions resolve from the inside outward, allowing a computed character name or other result to become an outer variable key or value. That order is behaviorally important because nested random choices or state-changing macros can affect the consumer rather than remaining inert text.
- **H2 Scoped blocks and whitespace preservation** (`scoped-blocks-and-whitespace-preservation`)
  Scoped blocks and whitespace preservation lets any argument-taking macro receive its final argument from text between opening and closing tags. The slash flag closes the named block. Default processing trims leading and trailing whitespace and removes shared indentation based on the first non-empty line, making multiline prose readable in source. The hash flag on the opening form preserves every newline and indentation character. Scoped content is semantically equivalent to an inline final argument after any earlier arguments and is appropriate for long or multiline values.
- **H2 Conditions, inversion, and else** (`conditions-inversion-and-else`)
  Conditions, inversion, and else specifies `if` blocks over no-argument macro names, nested expressions, variable shorthands, or literal values. Empty text, false, zero, off, and no are falsy. Prefixing the tested value with exclamation inverts the result, while `else` selects an alternate block when the first branch is false. The exclamation inside an `if` condition is logically distinct from the separately documented but unimplemented immediate flag, so parser context must be retained when explaining or editing the expression.
- **H2 Flags, comments, and escaping** (`flags-comments-and-escaping`)
  Flags, comments, and escaping separates two implemented flags from four planned forms. Slash closes a scoped block and hash preserves whitespace. Immediate exclamation, delayed question mark, re-evaluate tilde, and filter greater-than are documented plans, not current syntax. Single-line and scoped comments produce no output, with the scoped closing form using three slashes. Backslashes escape literal braces so authored text can display a macro-shaped token without resolving it. Kit must not advertise planned flags or accidentally unescape literal source.
- **H2 Variable scopes and identifier rules** (`variable-scopes-and-identifier-rules`)
  Variable scopes and identifier rules assigns dot-prefixed shorthand to chat-local storage and dollar-prefixed shorthand to installation-global storage. The characters are prefixes rather than flags or parts of the stored name. Standard identifiers begin with a letter, continue with letters, digits, underscores, or hyphens, and cannot end in underscore or hyphen. Nonconforming keys require full variable macros. Assigned values may contain nested macros, and whitespace around operators is ignored. Both scopes support the same operators but differ materially in persistence and reach.
- **H2 Variable shorthand operator table** (`variable-shorthand-operator-table`)
  Variable shorthand operator table records all operations and return rules. Get returns the value; set, add, and subtract mutate and return empty text; increment and decrement return the new value. Add performs numeric addition or non-numeric string concatenation, while subtract requires numbers. Falsy and undefined fallbacks differ, as do their assignment variants, and fallback expressions evaluate lazily. Equality uses strings and treats missing, null, and empty alike; greater and less comparisons are numeric. Every comparison returns literal true or false text suitable for `if`.
- **H2 Full variable macros and shorthand equivalence** (`full-variable-macros-and-shorthand-equivalence`)
  Full variable macros and shorthand equivalence maps dot and dollar get, set, add, increment, and decrement expressions to their named local and global macro forms. It preserves return behavior while showing that the prefix selects storage scope. Existence and deletion use `hasvar`, `deletevar`, `hasglobalvar`, and `deleteglobalvar`, since no shorthand operator replaces them. Comparison, fallback, and conditional-assignment shorthand also lack a one-token full-macro counterpart and should not be rewritten through an assumed mechanical equivalence.
- **H2 Legacy markers** (`legacy-markers`)
  Legacy markers maps USER to the user macro, BOT and CHAR to character, GROUP to group, and CHARIFNOTGROUP to the solo-character form. SillyTavern still recognizes these angle-bracket forms during processing for backward compatibility, but current authoring should use braces. Bunzai preserves legacy source during same-format round trips instead of performing cosmetic rewrites, because exact or tiered source stability may matter and another platform may interpret the text differently.
- **H2 Evaluation and safety contract** (`evaluation-and-safety-contract`)
  Evaluation and safety contract states that grammar validity does not prove a macro is registered in a particular field or processed at the expected stage. Variables, deletion, random choice, banned-word controls, and extension macros can alter state or generation behavior rather than merely returning text. Bunzai therefore treats every macro payload as sealed during import, inspection, conversion, and export and never runs it to infer meaning. Cross-format conversion preserves supported literal text, reports unrepresentable dialect behavior, and refuses name-only substitutions between superficially similar languages.

### SillyTavern documented macro catalog

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/macro-reference`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/macro-reference.md`
- Complete pinned catalog of every macro named in SillyTavern's official macro manual rather than a family-level sample. Authority and limits distinguishes the static documented set from extension-provided runtime registrations and field-specific availability. The catalog then enumerates participant, card, persona, message, swipe, clock, variable, random, model-state, prompt-template, Instruct, reasoning, image-generation, whitespace, input, banned-word, and World Info outlet macros with signatures, values, mutation behavior, and scope qualifications. Structural `if`, `else`, comment, block, and flag behavior routes to the operator reference. Runtime discovery requires `/? macros` or autocomplete for one live installation while using this page for pinned documented semantics. It warns that a known name is not proof of universal field support and that state-changing expressions remain sealed in Bunzai during import, inspection, conversion, and export.

- **H2 Catalog authority and limits** (`catalog-authority-and-limits`)
  Catalog authority and limits defines completeness against the pinned official macro page, not against every future extension or local installation. SillyTavern's runtime registry can add names, while `/? macros` and autocomplete expose the live set and arguments. The tables use recommended modern signatures and qualify scope, return value, or mutation. Because fields are processed at different stages, catalog membership does not guarantee resolution everywhere. Bunzai treats state-changing or otherwise executable macro text as sealed and does not evaluate it during content operations.
- **H2 Names, participants, cards, and personas** (`names-participants-cards-and-personas`)
  Names, participants, cards, and personas individually catalogs user, character, group, non-muted group, solo-character, and other-participant values, then all documented card and persona fields. It distinguishes description, personality, scenario, persona, character prompt overrides, depth note, creator notes, version, formatted and raw examples, greetings, alternate greeting index, and original override composition. Scope notes preserve group and solo behavior, metadata distinctions, and empty-value cases. The section also warns that character-to-persona conversion may reverse `user` and `char` roles rather than safely retaining literal tokens.
- **H2 Chat history, messages, time, and date** (`chat-history-messages-time-and-date`)
  Chat history, messages, time, and date enumerates latest message, latest user or character message, message indexes, context-window start, displayed-window start, current and final swipe indexes, full chat range, and extension-provided summary. Clock values cover local and UTC-offset time, short date, weekday, ISO time and date, custom formatting, idle duration, and two-value time difference. These results are runtime state rather than portable card data and can change across previews, branches, generations, installations, context fitting, display windows, and local clocks.
- **H2 Variables, randomization, and runtime state** (`variables-randomization-and-runtime-state`)
  Variables, randomization, and runtime state catalogs complete local and global variable operations: get, set, add or append, increment, decrement, existence, and delete, with mutation and return behavior. Random rerolls from a list, pick stays stable for its chat position until rerolled, and roll evaluates dice syntax. Runtime lookups expose prompt, context, and response token limits, selected model, mobile state, latest generation kind, and extension availability. The section stresses that random is not a numeric range and that global writes affect installation state beyond one conversation.
- **H2 Prompt, Instruct, reasoning, and image templates** (`prompt-instruct-reasoning-and-image-templates`)
  Prompt, Instruct, reasoning, and image templates catalogs the active and default system prompts, active, character, and default Author's Notes, Story String wrappers, user, assistant, and system role wrappers, separator, first and last role variants, stop sequence, filler, system-instruction prefix, example-chat separator, and chat-start marker. It also names reasoning prefix, suffix, and separator plus character positive and negative image-generation prompt prefixes. These values belong to model assembly stages and cannot be flattened into character identity or assumed equivalent to similarly named fields on another platform.
- **H2 Utility and World Info values** (`utility-and-world-info-values`)
  Utility and World Info values enumerates single and counted newlines, single and counted spaces, empty noop output, newline trimming, string reversal, current input, backend-dependent banned words, and named World Info outlets. It distinguishes text-returning utilities from actions with backend effects. Outlet lookup is case-sensitive and evaluation-stage dependent: content remains withheld until a compatible later field requests its exact key, and the form does not resolve inside World Info content, early card fields, or Author's Note. Outlet keys are therefore not automatically portable.
- **H2 Conditional and structural forms** (`conditional-and-structural-forms`)
  Conditional and structural forms identifies `if`, `else`, and comment expressions as language control forms rather than value lookups. Their complete truthiness, inversion, block, whitespace, and escape rules live in the operator reference. The section distinguishes implemented closing and preserve-whitespace flags from planned immediate, delayed, re-evaluate, and filter flags. Kit must not infer that planned syntax works merely because the official manual names it, and must keep structural meaning alongside the ordinary macro catalog.
- **H2 Runtime discovery rule** (`runtime-discovery-rule`)
  Runtime discovery rule tells Kit how to answer without confusing a pinned manual with live installation state. For a cataloged name, the reference supplies documented semantics. For existence or support in the user's installation or field, Kit should direct them to `/? macros` or field autocomplete because extensions and field registries change the available set. Claims must be labeled as pinned documentation or observed runtime behavior, and absent snapshot evidence must not be converted into certainty about a user's environment.

### SillyTavern macro language

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/macros`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/macros.md`
- Explains SillyTavern macros as a runtime expression language used across prompts, cards, World Info, Quick Replies, regex configuration, and scripts, with availability depending on field and evaluation stage. It routes exhaustive lookup to the companion documented-name catalog and complete operator reference. Core syntax covers double-brace names, arguments, nesting, scoped blocks, whitespace, conditionals, comments, escaping, and implemented versus planned flags. Variables describes chat-local and installation-global stores, full and shorthand operations, lazy fallbacks, and state mutation. Value families span participants, card and persona fields, runtime and random values, prompt templates, utilities, and extension-provided registrations. Portability hazards include order-dependent outlets, persona role reversal, regex substitution, script contexts, legacy markers, and platform semantic differences. Bunzai preserves exact source and keeps macros sealed rather than evaluating or translating them through superficial replacement.

- **H2 Core syntax and composition** (`core-syntax-and-composition`)
  Core syntax and composition defines modern case-insensitive double-brace macros, a space for one argument, and `::` for multiple arguments; single-colon syntax is legacy. Whitespace around names and separators is ignored, inner expressions resolve before outer consumers, and scoped blocks provide a final dedented argument unless `#` preserves whitespace. `{{if}}` supports truthiness, inversion, and else branches; comments and backslash escapes preserve non-output or literal braces. Only closing `/` and whitespace-preserving `#` flags are implemented in the pinned source, so Kit must not present documented future immediate, delayed, re-evaluate, or filter flags as available.
- **H2 Variables and state** (`variables-and-state`)
  Variables and state separates chat-local variables from installation-global variables. Full macros can get, set, add, increment, decrement, test existence, and delete values in either scope, with values sometimes containing JSON-like data. The experimental shorthand uses `.name` locally and `$name` globally and supports assignment, arithmetic or string addition, fallbacks, conditional assignment, equality, inequality, and numeric comparisons; fallback operands resolve lazily. Because expressions such as setvar mutate stored state during expansion, macro-bearing text is not necessarily passive. Bunzai never expands it while importing, inspecting, summarizing, converting, or exporting.
- **H2 Common value families** (`common-value-families`)
  Common value families groups macros by purpose. Participant values identify user, character, groups, and speakers. Card and persona values expose definitions, prompt overrides, notes, versions, examples, and greetings. Runtime values expose messages, swipes, time, model limits, generation type, extension state, and summaries. Random forms provide rerolled choice, stable chat choice, and dice. Template values expose active system and Author's Note text plus Instruct wrappers, stops, and reasoning markers, while utilities manipulate whitespace, strings, input, banned words, or named World Info outlets. Extensions can register more, so runtime help and field autocomplete remain authoritative for one installation.
- **H2 Evaluation and portability hazards** (`evaluation-and-portability-hazards`)
  Evaluation and portability hazards explains that the same valid macro may behave differently by processing phase. World Info outlets do not resolve within early character fields, World Info content, or Author's Note. Character-to-persona conversion may reverse user and character roles, regex fields choose disabled, raw, or escaped macro substitution, and scripts provide pipe or variable contexts absent from prompt prose. Platforms that share brace syntax can still disagree on names, arguments, truthiness, and timing. Bunzai preserves same-format text exactly, reports unsupported cross-format behavior, avoids execution, and retains escaped, nested, or legacy markers required by the source round-trip tier.

### SillyTavern personas and Data Bank

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/personas-and-data-bank`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/personas-and-data-bank.md`
- Explains two user-controlled SillyTavern context systems that differ in ownership and portability: personas represent the user's chat identity, while Data Bank documents provide retrievable reference material. Persona identity and description covers display metadata, prompt-bearing description, placement choices, and the limited character-to-persona conversion with macro-role reversal. Persona connections and backups describes chat locks, character associations, defaults, temporary selection, immutable historical attribution, explicit synchronization, backup omissions, and Bunzai's selected-persona import plus whole-source escrow. Data Bank documents and scopes distinguishes global, character, chat, and message-level material, inventories extractable text sources, and rejects the assumption that local attachments travel with a card. Vectorization and retrieval separates authored documents from generated, model-specific embedding indexes and identifies chunking, overlap, thresholds, query window, and insertion limits as retrieval controls. Bunzai can explain these systems and preserve supported persona semantics, but Data Bank is not yet a canonical entity and neither system should be silently folded into a character.

- **H2 Persona identity and description** (`persona-identity-and-description`)
  Persona identity and description defines a persona as display name, avatar, optional title, and optional prompt-bearing description. The title remains UI metadata. Description placement is saved per persona and may be disabled, expanded through Story String or Prompt Manager, placed around Author's Note, or injected into chat history at a chosen depth and role; therefore position must remain structured metadata. Character-to-persona conversion copies only useful identity fields, not scenario, personality, or greetings, and may require swapping `{{user}}` with `{{char}}` because their roles reverse.
- **H2 Persona connections and backups** (`persona-connections-and-backups`)
  Persona connections and backups defines selection precedence: a chat lock wins, then character association, then default persona, while a temporary choice lasts only until reopening. Past user messages retain their original persona attribution; switching identity does not rewrite history, and synchronization is a separate action. Optional multiple connections require a choice. Backups include names, descriptions, and character connections but omit images and chat connections, carry internal links, and are not public interchange packages. Bunzai imports one selected persona while escrowing the complete backup for same-format restoration.
- **H2 Data Bank documents and scopes** (`data-bank-documents-and-scopes`)
  Data Bank documents and scopes treats documents as retrieval sources rather than card fields. Global attachments apply everywhere, character attachments apply while that character participates, and chat attachments belong only to the active conversation; character attachments remain local and do not export with the card. Sources may be authored notes, extractable local files, readable web pages, video transcripts, or extension scrapers. PDF text, HTML, Markdown, ePUB, structured text, and source code can be converted when plain text is extractable, while unreadable binary input is rejected. A one-message file attachment does not imply a durable Data Bank entry.
- **H2 Vectorization and retrieval** (`vectorization-and-retrieval`)
  Vectorization and retrieval describes Vector Storage as a generated index over document chunks. A selected embedding provider turns chunks into model-specific vectors, and recent messages form a query that retrieves related chunks for prompt insertion. Chunk size, overlap, score threshold, query-message count, and insertion limits affect recall, precision, and context cost; changing source content or embedding model requires recalculation. Vectors are cached indexes stored separately from authored documents, not portable source truth. Bunzai should preserve source documents and supported retrieval settings separately, and must not claim current character import includes Data Bank because no dedicated canonical Data Bank entity exists.

### SillyTavern prompts and presets

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/prompts-and-presets`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/prompts-and-presets.md`
- Explains SillyTavern prompt construction as layered runtime assembly rather than one universal preset file. Prompt layers and precedence covers main or system instructions, character definitions and overrides, persona, World Info, Data Bank, examples, history, Author's Note, current input, and post-history instructions. Text Completion separates Context Template Story String from Instruct wrapping, while Chat Completion Prompt Manager models ordered role-bearing messages. Preset boundaries distinguish sampler presets, Context and Instruct templates, system prompts, Prompt Manager configurations, Quick Replies, and extension settings. CFG is multi-scope generation control. Tokenizer selection distinguishes overridable Text Completion from provider-bound Chat Completion, downloadable fallback, and Token Padding including negative values and trimming risk. Reasoning coverage includes token cost, display, acquisition, reinsertion, regex ephemerality, and provider effort controls. These are upstream product semantics; current Bunzai adapter capabilities remain governed by the format reference.

- **H2 Prompt layers and precedence** (`prompt-layers-and-precedence`)
  Prompt layers and precedence inventories the runtime request: general instructions, character and persona definitions, World Info, Data Bank retrieval, summaries, external results, example dialogue, chat history, the current user message, and final instructions. Position and role affect priority as much as prose. Main Prompt or System Prompt supplies broad behavior, while Post-History Instructions near generation commonly exert stronger immediate pressure. Character cards may replace either when user preferences permit, and the original macro composes the global value into a character override rather than fully replacing it. Author's Note can sit after Scenario or inside history at a selected depth and frequency. When a distant system change fails against established history, authors should open a new chat, repair contradictory turns, use examples, or place a narrow reminder closer to the generation boundary.
- **H2 Text Completion templates** (`text-completion-templates`)
  Text Completion templates are governed by Advanced Formatting. A Context Template Story String arranges system prompt, character description, personality, scenario, persona, and other macro-expanded components. Instruct Mode wraps story, system, user, and assistant content in model-specific sequences and can define story-string prefix and suffix, role prefixes and suffixes, first or last message variants, separators, stop sequences, and alignment filler. Include Names and wrapping options decide whether participant labels are authored into the serialized request. These values describe wire formatting for a model, not character identity. Custom stopping strings and reply prefill are adjacent generation controls: a stop array may be provider-limited, while Start Reply With may become a final text prefix or assistant-role prefill. A portable preset must keep these controls typed rather than concatenating them into one prompt block.
- **H2 Chat Completion Prompt Manager** (`chat-completion-prompt-manager`)
  Chat Completion Prompt Manager represents context as ordered messages with roles rather than one flat string. Utility prompts include format templates, new-chat and new-example markers, group nudges, continuation nudges, and empty-message replacements. Editable entries carry a name, role, trigger conditions, enabled state, position, depth, and order, and may sit at a relative position or an in-chat depth. The manager also controls character-name handling, continuation postfix or prefill, system-message squashing, inline media, reasoning requests, function calling, and provider-dependent system-role use. The visible list is an assembly plan: removing, disabling, or reordering one entry changes the request even when every prose block stays identical. Bunzai must preserve ordered prompt blocks and their roles rather than flattening a Chat Completion preset into a single string.
- **H2 Preset and interoperability boundaries** (`preset-and-interoperability-boundaries`)
  Preset and interoperability boundaries separates sampler presets, Context Templates, Instruct templates, system prompts, Prompt Manager configurations, Quick Reply presets, and extension-specific settings as distinct artifacts even when the UI groups them. Similar UI adjacency does not make them one file format. Bunzai's canonical preset models sampler settings, prompt blocks, and groups where adapters provide a semantic mapping. Platform-only ordering or provider controls may remain in escrow for same-format export. When converting to another platform, report unsupported roles, triggers, template sequences, and extension settings instead of presenting a partially flattened prompt as lossless. Inspect the final prompt when diagnosing behavior: an authored block may be valid but absent because its trigger did not fire, its position was disabled, context budget removed it, or a character override replaced the global value.
- **H2 Classifier-free guidance (CFG)** (`classifier-free-guidance-cfg`)
  Classifier-free guidance (CFG) mixes differences between positive and negative guidance streams as LLM prompt mixing, not Stable Diffusion negative tags. Supported backends named by the pin include oobabooga textgen WebUI, NovelAI, and TabbyAPI. VRAM rises because more than one prompt is ingested; OOM recovery is reduce context, use a smaller model, or disable CFG. Access sits alongside Author's Note. Four scopes are Chat CFG, Character CFG, Global CFG (overrides model preset CFG), and CFG Advanced Settings (formerly Prompt Cascading) to combine scopes and set insertion depth. Guidance scale 1 is off and sends nothing CFG-related; scale above 1 increases strength; scale below 1 inverts emphasis toward the negative stream; about 1.5 is a suggested start. Positive and negative prompts are optional. Group chats remove Character CFG and add Use Character CFG Scales and Character Negatives. Insertion depth defaults to 1; depth 0 can dominate and is not recommended with cascading. Treat CFG as runtime generation control, not character-card prose, unless a format explicitly stores it.
- **H2 Tokenizer selection and padding** (`tokenizer-selection-and-padding`)
  Tokenizer selection and padding sources Usage Prompts tokenizer at the pin. Tokenizers split text into tokens roughly three to four characters. Best match follows API family. Text Completion is overridable NovelAI Clio NerdStash Kayra NerdStash v2 API tokenizer if supported else Llama KoboldAI Classic AI Horde Llama KoboldCpp model API. Chat Completion is non-overridable OpenAI tiktoken Claude WebTokenizers OpenRouter families Gemma downloadable models fallback GPT-3.5 turbo. Text Completion overrides include None rounded up Llama Llama 3 NerdStash Mistral Yi Gemma DeepSeek API tokenizer ooba koboldcpp TabbyAPI Aphrodite. Downloadable Qwen2 Command-R Mistral V3 Nemo DeepSeek chat download once unless enableDownloadableTokenizers false with manual JSON under data cache Llama 3 fallback when uncached downloads disabled. Token Padding is Text Completion only; Chat Completion always matches its tokenizer. Padding reserves room near max context so definitions are not truncated. Negative padding can exceed stated max tokens and raise prompt-trimming risk.
- **H2 Reasoning and chain-of-thought blocks** (`reasoning-and-chain-of-thought-blocks`)
  Reasoning and chain-of-thought blocks notes that hidden reasoning can consume Max Response Length, so incomplete or empty answers often need 1024 to 4096 response tokens on reasoning models. Blocks appear collapsible; Auto-Expand and Show Hidden are Advanced Formatting options; edit and copy work when expanded. Adding reasoning covers manual lightbulb edits, extensions writing extra.reasoning, the reasoning-set command, backend Request model reasoning for named sources, and Auto-Parse with Prefix and Suffix for unparsed streams. Prompting with reasoning defaults to not resending thoughts, describes Add to Prompts wrapping with Max Additions, and notes Continue may resend incomplete reasoning-only messages. Regex and effort covers Regex Affects Reasoning, ephemerality modes, and Reasoning Effort Auto through Maximum as connection configuration mapped to provider budgets, never a character-card field.
  - **H3 Adding reasoning** (`adding-reasoning`)
    Adding reasoning can be Manual via the message edit lightbulb or extensions writing extra.reasoning; Command via reasoning-set with optional at message id defaulting to the last message; Backend via Request model reasoning in AI Response Configuration when the source supports it (for most sources this only toggles request where the API allows and does not fully disable thinking). Named pin sources include Claude, DeepSeek, Google AI Studio/Vertex, OpenRouter, xAI, AI/ML API, Z.AI, Pollinations, MistralAI, Electron Hub, Chutes, NanoGPT, and Moonshot, with source-specific disable and effort caveats. Parse uses Auto-Parse with Prefix and Suffix (DeepSeek-style think defaults) for unparsed streams such as MiniMax or Perplexity.
  - **H3 Prompting with reasoning** (`prompting-with-reasoning`)
    Prompting with reasoning defaults to not resending prior reasoning into later turns. Add to Prompts wraps content with Prefix, Suffix, and Separator; Max Additions counts from the end of the prompt. Providers often discourage multi-turn chain-of-thought resend. Continue may resend incomplete reasoning-only messages without requiring Add to Prompts so the model can finish thinking and then emit content. Authors must treat reinsertion as an explicit opt-in with token cost rather than an automatic memory of all hidden thoughts.
  - **H3 Regex and effort** (`regex-and-effort`)
    Regex and effort notes that Regex Affects can include Reasoning. Ephemerality modes are none for permanent rewrite, run-on-edit to re-apply, alter display only, and alter outgoing prompts only. Reasoning Effort in the Chat Completion AI Response panel maps Auto, Minimum, Low, Medium, High, and Maximum to provider-specific budgets or keywords (Claude token budgets or adaptive thinking; OpenAI, OpenRouter, xAI, Perplexity, and NanoGPT keywords; Google thinkingBudget or thinkingLevel tables for 2.5 and 3.x models). Auto usually omits the parameter. Effort is connection configuration, not a character-card field.

### SillyTavern authoring reference

- id: `reference/platforms/sillytavern/README`
- path: `docs/reference/platforms/sillytavern/README.md`
- Curated entry point for Kit's SillyTavern authoring knowledge. The collection covers portable and behaviorally significant content rather than installation, hosting, providers, or ordinary chat controls. Collection map routes character cards including sprites, chats and groups with JSONL versus TXT, World Info, personas and Data Bank, prompts and presets including CFG and reasoning, macro language semantics, an exhaustive pinned macro catalog, complete macro operators, regex and STscript automation, and an auditable source-coverage contract to focused pages, requiring whole-page reading around a search hit because prompt order, scope, and timing qualify isolated matches. Authority and provenance identifies independently organized Bunzai text, pins official SillyTavern-Docs commit 70e5e4d3c239253fca4692fe82e3936cb9c4b1b1 retrieved 2026-07-26 with the same pin as 2026-07-25, and preserves AGPL license and contributor links. How Kit should apply it separates upstream behavior, current adapter behavior, and authoring advice, making the format adapter page authoritative for implementation claims and forbidding invented adapter support from upstream alone. Refresh policy limits updates to content types, workflows, prompt contracts, or interoperability and requires revision comparison, regenerated semantic summaries, and independent review while excluding routine UI, install, and provider churn.

- **H2 Collection map** (`collection-map`)
  Collection map routes Characters card fields greetings advanced definitions metadata tags expression sprites permanent versus conditional prompt content Chats and groups re-importable chat JSONL versus lossy TXT checkpoints group reply strategies join modes mute force auto scenario overrides World Info lorebooks activation insertion recursion grouping timed effects outlets vector matching Personas and Data Bank user identities prompt placement connections backups attachment scopes retrieval documents Prompts and presets final request Text Completion templates Chat Completion Prompt Manager Author Note CFG reasoning blocks tokenizer selection and padding portable preset boundaries Macros evaluation Documented macro catalog Macro operators Regex and automation scripts STscript Quick Replies and Source coverage for every evaluated upstream authoring page. Use the narrow owning page then read surrounding constraints because features interact through prompt order scope or evaluation timing.
- **H2 Authority and provenance** (`authority-and-provenance`)
  Authority and provenance declares these pages independently organized Bunzai documentation rather than a verbatim mirror or upstream replacement. It pins SillyTavern/SillyTavern-Docs to commit 70e5e4d3c239253fca4692fe82e3936cb9c4b1b1 retrieved on 2026-07-26 (same pin as 2026-07-25), and states every page links its principal upstream sources. Upstream documentation is licensed under AGPL-3.0; Bunzai is also AGPL-3.0-or-later. Upstream copyright and contributor credits remain available in the official documentation repository, including its license file and LicenseCredits document. The pin, retrieval date, and attribution together make later audits and refreshes reproducible for Kit and maintainers.
- **H2 How Kit should apply it** (`how-kit-should-apply-it`)
